**[00:56:00.000] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-000245)  
Madam Speaker, before we proceed with the sponsorship and plenary deliberations on House Bill No. 10858, I move to recognize the Hon. Perci Cendaña of Akbayan Partylist for a brief manifestation.

**[00:56:16.220] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000246)  
The Hon. Perci Cendaña of Akbayan Partylist is hereby recognized for his manifestation.

**[00:56:21.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000247)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker. Magandang hapon po sa ating mga kasamahan dito sa kapulongan ito at sa mga kababayan nating nanonood ngayon, lalong-lalo na ang mga miyembro ng Civil Society Organization.

**[00:56:35.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000248)  
Madam Speaker, my dear colleagues, this representation would like to raise an important concern from our Civil Society Organization.

**[00:56:43.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000249)  
Last year po, nagkaroon tayo ng guidelines on CSO participation. Ito po ang chinampion ng ating good chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations.

**[00:56:51.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000250)  
Chair Mika Suansing. Hindi naman po ito bago. May ganito na rin pong effort noong 15 Congress in 2006.

**[00:57:00.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000251)  
It seems that while we are driving towards the right direction, this year, parang biglang nag-U-turn po tayo.

**[00:57:09.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000252)  
Our CSOs are sharing their frustration about how they are being treated in the budget process for the 2027 General Appropriations Bill.

**[00:57:18.580] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000253)  
Sa isang inquiry article,

**[00:57:21.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000254)  
nabanggit po ng mga CSO na sila daw ay na-seen zone, ni hindi nalaman that we adopted last year's guidelines until week 2 of our budget deliberations.

**[00:57:32.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000255)  
Tapos ngayon, parang naghahabol tayo to bring them and draw them in.

**[00:57:37.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000256)  
Kaya po ang tanong natin, Madam Speaker, my dear colleagues, aniyari?

**[00:57:43.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000257)  
Saan po tayo nagkaroon ng gap in terms of coordinating with our CSOs?

**[00:57:47.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000258)  
Wala na bang mas makabulang participation than their questions being read out?

**[00:57:51.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000259)  
Will CSOs be informed and involved at sa magiging amendments sa budget through the BAR-C?

**[00:57:57.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000260)  
And most importantly, nagkaroon lang po ba ng guidelines on CSO participation last year dahil nabuking ang flood control projects?

**[00:58:05.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000261)  
Sa totoo lamang po, Madam Speaker, napakahalagan na ang transparency in winning back the confidence of our people in the budget process in this House.

**[00:58:15.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000262)  
What we began last year was a very significant and important step and we have to learn from that.

**[00:58:21.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000263)  
And from the unfortunate experiences of our CSO representatives this year.

**[00:58:27.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000264)  
We hope that it's not too late for the House leadership to take this matter seriously and act on it.

**[00:58:32.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000265)  
Hindi na pwedeng ipalusot lang sa kanila na we're still learning.

**[00:58:36.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000266)  
Pera ng taong bayan, sa bulsa ng mamamayang Pilipino galing, ang pinag-uusapan nating P7.2 trillion na ito.

**[00:58:43.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000267)  
Sa dulo po, hayaan po natin ang meaningful participation ng civil society organizations to define the kind of budget,

**[00:58:51.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000268)  
that our country really needs.

**[00:58:53.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000269)  
Marami pong salamat, Madam Speaker.

**[00:58:57.300] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000270)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:58:58.620] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-000271)

It is a constitutional duty

in every season

and under every circumstance

**[01:08:42.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000547)  
Long after this plenary ends

**[01:08:44.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000548)

**[01:08:46.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000549)

**[01:08:48.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000550)

**[01:08:54.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000553)  
that we decided well

**[01:09:42.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000577)  
Para sa Diyos at bayan.

Minority Leader, the Honorable

**[01:17:14.760] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-000684)  
Marcelino C. Libanan

**[01:17:16.400] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-000685)  
from the 4Ps Party-list to interpellate

**[01:17:18.660] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-000686)  
the sponsor.

**[01:17:20.460] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000687)  
The brilliant legislator

**[01:17:22.720] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000688)  
from the province of Samar,

**[01:17:25.340] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000689)  
the Honorable Minority Leader

**[01:17:26.920] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000690)  
representing 4Ps

**[01:17:28.640] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000691)  
Party-list,

**[01:17:30.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000692)  
Nonoy Libanan is hereby recognized

**[01:17:31.400] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000693)  
for his interpellation.

**[01:17:33.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000694)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:17:38.140] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000695)  
And good afternoon

**[01:17:39.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000696)  
to our sponsor,

**[01:17:42.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000697)  
Honorable

**[01:17:43.260] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000698)  
Suansing and

**[01:17:44.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000699)  
the DBCC family.

**[01:17:48.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000700)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:17:50.360] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000701)  
before we discuss

**[01:17:51.460] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000702)  
individual departments,

**[01:17:53.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000703)  
may I begin with the principles

**[01:17:55.380] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000704)  
that should govern

**[01:17:57.260] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000705)  
this entire bill.

**[01:18:00.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000706)  
A national budget

**[01:18:01.160] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000707)  
is more than a table

**[01:18:03.560] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000708)  
of appropriations.

**[01:18:06.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000709)  
It tells us what government

**[01:18:07.740] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000710)  
considers important,

**[01:18:10.100] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000711)  
who bears the burden

**[01:18:11.520] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000712)  
when fiscal space

**[01:18:13.660] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000713)  
is tight, where development

**[01:18:16.040] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000714)  
will occur, and what

**[01:18:17.960] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000715)  
safeguards Congress

**[01:18:19.980] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000716)  
places around the people's

**[01:18:21.980] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000717)  
money.

**[01:18:23.480] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000718)  
There are provisions in this bill

**[01:18:25.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000719)  
that deserve recognition.

**[01:18:29.320] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000720)  
The significance

**[01:18:29.920] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000721)  
reduction in unprogrammed

**[01:18:32.440] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000722)  
appropriations is one.

**[01:18:35.160] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000723)  
Greater transparency

**[01:18:36.420] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000724)  
in those budget processes

**[01:18:37.980] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000725)  
is another.

**[01:18:40.300] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000726)  
But precisely

**[01:18:41.240] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000727)  
because Congress has improved

**[01:18:44.180] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000728)  
the process,

**[01:18:46.040] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000729)  
we should now ask the harder questions

**[01:18:48.080] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000730)  
about the substance.

**[01:18:51.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000731)  
How much of this

**[01:18:51.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000732)  
7.2 trillion

**[01:18:53.680] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000733)  
do we actually control?

**[01:18:57.280] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000734)  
Are appropriations

**[01:18:58.420] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000735)  
specific enough?

**[01:18:59.920] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000736)  
For Congress to know

**[01:19:01.420] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000737)  
where the money will go?

**[01:19:04.760] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000738)  
Do the special provisions

**[01:19:06.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000739)  
preserve congressional intent?

**[01:19:10.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000740)  
Are constitutional

**[01:19:12.560] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000741)  
priorities protected?

**[01:19:15.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000742)  
Is spending

**[01:19:16.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000743)  
geographically fair?

**[01:19:19.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000744)  
And what

**[01:19:21.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000745)  
measurable results

**[01:19:22.440] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000746)  
should the people expect

**[01:19:24.220] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000747)  
in return?

**[01:19:26.320] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000748)  
Those are the principles

**[01:19:28.280] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000749)  
by which the minority

**[01:19:29.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000750)  
proposes to examine this bill.

**[01:19:32.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000751)  
The General Appropriations Act

**[01:19:34.740] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000752)  
is not merely

**[01:19:36.340] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000753)  
a schedule of amounts.

**[01:19:38.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000754)  
It is the principal

**[01:19:40.460] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000755)  
instrument through which Congress

**[01:19:42.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000756)  
determines priorities,

**[01:19:44.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000757)  
imposes conditions

**[01:19:46.220] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000758)  
on expenditure,

**[01:19:48.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000759)  
safeguards public money,

**[01:19:50.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000760)  
and holds

**[01:19:51.660] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000761)  
executive accountable

**[01:19:54.440] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000762)  
for results.

**[01:19:58.200] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000763)  
Madam Sponsor,

**[01:19:59.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000764)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:20:01.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000765)  
would you agree

**[01:20:03.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000766)  
that Congress is not entirely free

**[01:20:07.360] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000767)  
to rank all expenditures equally

**[01:20:09.560] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000768)  
because the Constitution itself

**[01:20:12.460] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000769)  
establishes certain priorities,

**[01:20:15.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000770)  
most expressly

**[01:20:17.380] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000771)  
the command to assign

**[01:20:19.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000772)  
the highest budgetary priority

**[01:20:21.700] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000773)  
to education?

**[01:20:28.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000774)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, yes. Um,

**[01:20:31.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000777)  
however, in that particular instance,

**[01:20:33.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000778)  
it is actually for the benefit

**[01:20:35.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000779)  
of our countrymen that such

**[01:20:37.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000780)  
provision which states that education

**[01:20:39.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000781)  
has to be the highest budgetary

**[01:20:42.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000782)  
allocation,

**[01:20:43.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000783)  
it's actually beneficial to our countrymen.

**[01:20:47.760] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000784)  
Then, Madam Speaker,

**[01:20:49.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000785)  
when Congress

**[01:20:51.820] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000786)

**[01:20:53.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000787)  
not merely on whether education

**[01:20:55.640] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000788)  
receives the largest

**[01:20:57.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000789)  
nominal amount, but whether

**[01:20:59.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000790)  
our overall fiscal choices

**[01:21:01.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000791)  
preserve constitutional priorities

**[01:21:04.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000792)  
given to education

**[01:21:06.660] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000793)  
despite

**[01:21:08.100] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000794)

**[01:21:13.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000795)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:21:14.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000796)  
distinguished colleague,

**[01:21:15.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000797)  
yes, I agree with

**[01:21:18.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000798)  
our distinguished colleague.

**[01:21:20.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000799)  
However, when we look at the budget

**[01:21:21.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000800)  
and we determine

**[01:21:23.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000801)  
the allocations

**[01:21:26.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000802)  
for the different agencies,

**[01:21:28.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000803)  
we look at it at a holistic level.

**[01:21:29.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000804)  
But, yes, I agree, Madam Speaker.

**[01:21:33.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000805)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:21:33.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000806)  
I raised this question because

**[01:21:35.540] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000807)  
comparison between

**[01:21:37.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000808)  
the interest payment

**[01:21:39.760] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000809)  
and the budget

**[01:21:41.980] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000810)  
for DepEd

**[01:21:42.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000811)  
is 1.114 trillion

**[01:21:46.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000812)  
for interest payments

**[01:21:48.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000813)  
and

**[01:21:49.560] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000814)  
976 billion

**[01:21:51.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000815)  
for the Department

**[01:21:53.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000816)  
of Education.

**[01:21:55.920] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000817)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:21:56.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000818)  
would the sponsor

**[01:21:58.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000819)  
agree?

**[01:21:59.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000820)  
That, as a general rule,

**[01:22:02.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000821)  
Congress should prepare

**[01:22:04.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000822)  
specific itemized appropriations

**[01:22:07.240] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000823)  
over broad lump sums

**[01:22:09.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000824)  
whenever the expenditure

**[01:22:11.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000825)  
can reasonably be identified

**[01:22:13.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000826)  
in advance?

**[01:22:16.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000827)  
Yes, Madam Speaker,

**[01:22:18.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000828)  
distinguished colleague, I agree.

**[01:22:19.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000829)  
Where possible, we would prefer

**[01:22:22.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000830)  
specific line items over lump sum amounts.

**[01:22:26.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000831)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker,

**[01:22:26.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000832)  
for that answer.

**[01:22:28.040] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000833)  
But, Madam Speaker,

**[01:22:29.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000834)  
there are items

**[01:22:31.260] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000835)  
which we cannot really calculate.

**[01:22:33.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000836)  
Example, calamities.

**[01:22:36.540] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000837)  
We shall have

**[01:22:37.300] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000838)  
leeway for that.

**[01:22:41.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000839)  
It cannot be predicted.

**[01:22:43.920] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000840)  
But roads, bridges,

**[01:22:46.640] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000841)  
buildings, and other

**[01:22:47.600] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000842)  
identified infrastructure

**[01:22:50.080] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000843)  
normally can be.

**[01:22:53.540] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000844)  
Can the sponsor

**[01:22:54.320] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000845)  
therefore assure the House

**[01:22:55.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000846)  
that broad lump sums

**[01:22:57.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000847)  
have not simply been

**[01:22:58.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000848)  
migrated from

**[01:23:00.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000849)  
unprogrammed appropriations

**[01:23:02.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000850)  
into programmed appropriations

**[01:23:04.980] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000851)  
or special provision?

**[01:23:21.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000852)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:23:22.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000853)  
Distinguished colleague,

**[01:23:23.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000854)  
with regard to that particular instance,

**[01:23:25.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000855)  
in response to the calamities,

**[01:23:27.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000856)  
we do have

**[01:23:28.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000857)  
the NDRRMF, which at the moment has an allocation of 45.67 billion. While that is a lump sum amount, we do have within it QRF allocations for the different agencies.

**[01:23:44.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000858)  
In that case, given that it needs to be flexible to be able to respond to whichever agencies, calamities, or activities would need to be funded at a particular time, for this particular instance, a lump sum amount is actually preferable for the NDRRMF.

**[01:24:07.540] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000859)  
Thank you for that, Madam Speaker.

**[01:24:10.420] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000860)  
Because our country is most often visited by typhoons and catastrophes.

**[01:24:18.080] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000861)  
Would the sponsor agree that a special provision should implement an appropriation approved by Congress and should not effectively create a new spending authority that is broader than the appropriation itself?

**[01:24:44.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000862)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, a special provision sets the condition by which an appropriation can be disbursed.

**[01:24:58.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000863)  
So, a special provision emanates from the appropriation itself.

**[01:25:02.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000864)  
So, in theory, technically speaking, a special provision would not supersede a particular appropriation.

**[01:25:09.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000865)  
And this conforms to our Constitution and applicable law.

**[01:25:14.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000866)  
Madam Speaker.

**[01:25:15.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000867)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:25:17.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000868)  
Then, can the sponsor assure this Chamber that there is no provision in this bill that allows funds appropriated for one clearly defined purpose to be redirected to an unrelated purpose without returning to Congress?

**[01:26:07.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000869)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, in general, that is the case.

**[01:26:10.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000870)  
However, there is a general provision within the

**[01:26:14.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000871)  
General Appropriations Bill which allows for the declaration and use of savings, which is guided by Supreme Court jurisprudence.

**[01:26:22.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000872)  
But generally, aside from that particular case, generally, whichever intended use is there when the GAA was approved should be eventually the use for a particular fund.

**[01:26:44.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000873)  
Madam Speaker, we can also probably revisit on augmentation, on fund transfers, on GOCC remittances, foreign assisted fund projects, lump sum infrastructure,

**[01:27:00.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000874)  
LGU support funds, and release of unprogrammed appropriations.

**[01:27:05.280] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000875)  
Madam Speaker.

**[01:27:22.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000876)  
Madam Speaker, my apologies.

**[01:27:24.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000877)  
May I clarify if there is a question from the Honorable Minority Leader, or is it more of an example of the different kinds of funds?

**[01:27:32.300] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000878)  
It's just to revisit like your assurance that the Madam Sponsor gave us about the use of savings.

**[01:27:40.480] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000879)  
Perhaps we can revisit the appropriate

**[01:27:44.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000880)  
laws on this so that, if possible, we go back to Congress for appropriation.

**[01:28:50.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000882)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, after consultation with DBM, for those particular items, generally it is governed by the general provision on the Declaration and Use of Savings.

**[01:29:02.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000883)  
However, upon conferring with the DBM, what they can assure is that they will make sure that they will strictly adhere to whichever conditions are there that is set forth in the general provision for the use of savings.

**[01:29:16.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000884)  
So there will, because that particular general provision is also authorized by Congress, but what they can assure, what the DBM can assure is that those funds will not be reallocated.

**[01:29:32.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000885)  
They will not be reallocated or augmented without adherence to the particular conditions set forth in the general provision.

**[01:29:43.280] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000886)  
Thank you for that assurance, Madam Speaker.

**[01:29:45.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000887)  
Madam Speaker, of the 7.2 trillion before us, how much is truly discretionary and how much is already committed by automatic appropriations, statutory transfers?

**[01:30:00.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000888)  
personal obligations, and debt service.

**[01:30:04.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000889)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[01:30:07.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000890)  
in terms of the split for the 7.2 trillion pesos,

**[01:30:11.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000891)  
4.494 trillion is under programmed appropriations

**[01:30:15.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000892)  
and 2.706 trillion is under automatic appropriations.

**[01:30:21.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000893)  
So on the part of Congress,

**[01:30:24.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000894)  
in the General Appropriations Bill,

**[01:30:26.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000895)  
we are unable to touch the automatic appropriations.

**[01:30:29.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000896)  
So we are looking at the 4.494 trillion pesos

**[01:30:33.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000897)  
under programmed appropriations.

**[01:30:35.920] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000898)  
That's why this question, Madam Speaker,

**[01:30:37.980] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000899)  
because we are only appropriating 4.9 trillion

**[01:30:41.320] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000900)  
in comparison to the 7.2 trillion

**[01:30:45.300] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000901)  
we are releasing in our headlines, Madam Speaker.

**[01:30:50.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000902)  
So it's only 4.9 trillion.

**[01:30:53.700] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000903)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:30:55.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000904)  
would the sponsor agree

**[01:30:57.160] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000905)  
that an appropriation

**[01:30:59.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000906)  
is not merely an accomplishment?

**[01:31:01.760] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000907)  
That the proper measure

**[01:31:03.460] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000908)  
is whether the money is obligated,

**[01:31:06.700] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000909)  
disbursed,

**[01:31:07.680] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000910)  
and produces the promised physical

**[01:31:10.320] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000911)  
or social result?

**[01:31:13.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000912)  
I agree, Madam Speaker.

**[01:31:15.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000913)  
I agree completely

**[01:31:16.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000914)  
with our Honorable Minority Leader.

**[01:31:18.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000915)  
The gauge really is the impact

**[01:31:20.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000916)  
of these items.

**[01:31:23.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000917)  
What would be the benefit

**[01:31:24.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000918)  
that these allocations would give

**[01:31:26.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000919)  
to our countrymen?

**[01:31:27.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000920)  
They are not simply

**[01:31:28.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000921)  
numbers on a page.

**[01:31:31.520] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000922)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:31:32.740] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000923)  
I would like to emphasize this,

**[01:31:34.100] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000924)  
that this is only the beginning,

**[01:31:36.260] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000925)  
but the more important

**[01:31:38.540] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000926)  
benefit of appropriation

**[01:31:41.200] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000927)  
is implementation.

**[01:31:45.160] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000928)  
Implementation of this

**[01:31:46.760] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000929)  
appropriated fund.

**[01:31:48.980] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000930)  
So that this will redound

**[01:31:50.700] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000931)  
really to the benefit

**[01:31:52.760] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000932)  
of our people.

**[01:31:55.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000933)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:32:02.440] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000934)  
Then should agencies receiving major increases be required to show their obligation, disbursement, and physical accomplishment rates before Congress increases their budget?

**[01:32:25.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000935)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:32:29.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000936)  
Distinguished colleague, yes, that is the case.

**[01:32:32.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000937)  
There is also an assessment

**[01:32:34.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000938)  
of the utilization rate, especially

**[01:32:36.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000939)  
for agencies that receive high

**[01:32:38.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000940)  
allocations.

**[01:32:39.900] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000941)  
Madam Speaker, we shall see to it

**[01:32:42.440] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000942)  
that if there is

**[01:32:43.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000943)  
low utilization,

**[01:32:47.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000944)  
we shall not be increasing

**[01:32:50.280] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000945)  
their budget, Madam Speaker.

**[01:32:52.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000946)  
And like the

**[01:32:53.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000947)  
2027 budget,

**[01:32:56.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000948)  
we did not allow them to

**[01:32:58.040] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000949)  
increase their budget because of

**[01:32:59.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000950)  
low utilization

**[01:33:02.160] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000951)  
rate. Madam

**[01:33:04.260] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000952)  
Speaker, would the sponsor

**[01:33:06.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000953)  
agree that the national budget

**[01:33:08.480] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000954)  
financed by all Filipinos

**[01:33:10.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000955)  
should reasonably

**[01:33:12.180] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000956)  
distribute development

**[01:33:14.320] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000957)  
opportunities throughout the country?

**[01:33:17.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000958)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:33:21.040] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000959)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:33:22.920] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000960)  
I agree.

**[01:33:23.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000961)  
That equality

**[01:33:25.760] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000962)  
does not mean

**[01:33:27.540] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000963)  
identical amounts.

**[01:33:30.100] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000964)  
We cannot talk of identical amounts,

**[01:33:32.160] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000965)  
but should the criteria

**[01:33:33.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000966)  
not include poverty,

**[01:33:36.200] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000967)  
infrastructure deficits,

**[01:33:37.880] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000968)  
geographic isolation,

**[01:33:40.220] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000969)  
and disaster exposure,

**[01:33:42.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000970)  
not merely on

**[01:33:44.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000971)  
existing economic activity

**[01:33:46.200] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000972)  
and project

**[01:33:47.780] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000973)  
readiness. Would you agree,

**[01:35:03.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000997)

**[01:35:05.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000998)

**[01:35:07.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000999)

**[01:35:19.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001005)

16.1% po

mga

to implement these projects.

**[01:54:47.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001382)  
National

**[01:54:48.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001383)  
Heritage, meron po tayong

**[01:54:50.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001384)  
ahensya para dyan.

**[01:55:42.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001390)  
Madam Speaker, meron po kasing

**[01:55:44.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001391)  
mga local

**[01:55:46.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001392)  
government sites, ay mga local

**[01:55:48.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001393)  
sites,

**[01:55:48.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001394)  
na actually po hindi po nakapaloob doon po sa pinanghahawakan ng NHCP.

**[01:55:56.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001395)  
So in cases like that po, may mga pagkakataon po na nag-request po yung mga LGUs.

**[01:56:02.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001396)  
For example po, Madam Speaker, ano po ba yung mga hindi nasasakop ng cultural heritage, rehabilitation and repair ng ahensya para sa National Heritage Commission?

**[01:56:32.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001397)  
Isa pong halimbawa na binigay po ng DBM ay yung Old City Hall ng Cotabato.

**[01:56:40.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001398)  
Ito po kasi ay kinonvert na po sa isang museum.

**[01:56:44.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001399)  
So isa po ito sa mga halimbawa na inirequest po sa kanila ng Cotabato City Government na mapondohan po sa ilalim po ng menu item B po.

**[01:56:56.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001400)  
Ito po ba ay nadeklara ng heritage site?

**[01:57:02.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001401)  
Opo, nadeklara na po.

**[01:57:04.440] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001402)  
Bakit hindi po po pwedeng pondohan ang National Heritage Commission?

**[01:57:09.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001403)  
Kasi po ito po ay isang, bagamat ito po ay nadeklara na bilang isang heritage site, nanatili po ito bilang isang LGU property.

**[01:57:19.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001404)  
Kaya po ang nag-maintain pa rin po ay yung local government unit.

**[01:57:25.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001405)  
Sa pagkakaalam ko, may parameters sa pagkukumpuni, pagre-rehab.

**[01:57:32.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001406)  
At ang mga heritage site at ang tanging authority dyan ay ang national heritage.

**[01:57:42.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001407)  
Madam Speaker, may mga pagkakataon po kasi na hindi naman po ganun kalakihan yung budget ng NHCP.

**[01:57:48.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001408)  
So hindi din po nila na-accommodate lahat po ng mga nadeklara bilang heritage site.

**[01:57:54.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001409)  
So may mga pagkakataon po na mismo yung mga local government units na po yung nag-initiate ng request sa ilalim po ng LGU.

**[01:58:01.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001410)  
Para po mapondohan yung ganitong mga maintenance po or restoration ng mga heritage sites.

**[01:58:07.820] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001411)  
So ibig pong sabihin, lahat na nakapaloob dito sa mga parameters neto, pwede nang hindi humingi sa mga departamento at mga ahensya ng national government at pwede nang dumiretsyo sa office of the president.

**[01:58:18.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001412)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:58:22.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001413)  
Opo, mayroon po itong mga complement sa iba't ibang mga programa ng mga ahensya ng gobyerno.

**[01:58:31.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001414)  
However, Pai, you just said it.

**[01:58:31.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001415)  
I just want to clarify po, the request is not directed to the president but rather it's directed to DBM through a portal.

**[01:58:39.620] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001416)  
DBM is the alter ego of the president.

**[01:58:42.900] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001417)  
Pag pinadaan sa DBM, ang request po kay presidente, hindi sa DBM.

**[01:58:48.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001418)  
The approval po, the evaluation and the approval stops at the level of DBM.

**[01:58:55.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001419)  
Hindi na po ito kailangang iakyat po sa office of the president.

**[01:59:00.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001420)  
Construction, concreting, rehab.

**[01:59:01.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001421)  
Repair of local roads, bridges, public markets, multi-purpose buildings, multi-purpose halls.

**[01:59:09.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001422)  
Hindi po ba trabaho ito ng Department of Public Works and Highways?

**[01:59:15.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001423)  
Di ba po ito, yan yung pinag-aawayan dito sa Kongreso kung saan may mga allocables ang mga kinatawan?

**[01:59:22.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001424)  
Ngayon po, inililipat na lang sa mga local governments?

**[01:59:26.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001425)  
Yes.

**[01:59:28.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001426)  
Paano natin masisiguro na equitable ang distribution?

**[01:59:31.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001427)  
Ang mga pondong ito?

**[02:00:00.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001428)  
Madam Speaker, I agree with the Honorable Erice.

**[02:00:06.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001429)  
Actually, opo, tama po yun. Meron din pong component yung DPWH na Basic Infrastructure Program or SIPAG

**[02:00:14.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001430)  
kung saan pwede pong mapondohan ng DPWH yun po mga local roads, yung mga multipurpose buildings sa iba't ibang mga lugar po.

**[02:00:23.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001431)  
Pero binibigyan pa rin po ito dahil po kasama siya doon sa menu na na-aprobahan ng Kongreso.

**[02:00:32.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001432)  
Pwede po itong i-request sa ilalim po ng LGSF.

**[02:00:35.480] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001433)  
Okay po. Para pa ang patunayan natin, Madam Speaker, Madam Chair, kung equitable yung distribution nitong LGSF.

**[02:00:45.020] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001434)  
Nakapag-release na po ba sila o sa mga local governments ng 2025, 37 billion po ang LGSF.

**[02:00:53.860] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001435)  
May mga na-release na po ba dyan? O naubos na yan?

**[02:01:04.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001436)  
Madam Speaker, opo, meron na pong ganong listahan.

**[02:01:08.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001437)  
Ayon po sa DBM, maaari po itong makita doon sa Compass na portal sa DBM website.

**[02:01:16.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001438)  
Nandun na po yung listahan ng mga releases sa ilalim po ng LGSF so far for 2025.

**[02:01:23.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001439)  
Fiscal year 2026 po.

**[02:01:27.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001440)  
How about 2025?

**[02:01:29.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001441)  
Mula po 2022 hanggang so far po, half or until August of 2026.

**[02:01:37.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001442)  
Ano po ang utilization rate ng LGSF 2025 and 2026?

**[02:02:16.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001443)  
Sa taong 2025 po,

**[02:02:21.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001444)  
ang LGSF,

**[02:02:23.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001445)  
Ang released ay 59.74%, ang obligated po ay 13.01%, at ang disbursed po ay 4.15%.

**[02:02:34.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001446)  
Sa 2026 po, as of July 22, ang released po ay 82.73%, obligated ay 20.2%, at yung disbursement po ay 43.1%.

**[02:02:48.120] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001447)  
Ano po ang ano? 2026, ano po ang utilization rate?

**[02:02:50.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001448)  
May...

**[02:02:51.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001449)  
Anong gamitin natin?

**[02:02:53.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001450)  
Ang basehan na lang po natin, Madam Speaker, yung released amount.

**[02:03:03.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001451)  
So sa 2025 po, 59.74% out of the 18.95 billion pesos na allocation.

**[02:03:13.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001452)  
At sa 2026 po, 82.73% out of the 37.49 billion allocation.

**[02:03:20.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001453)  
Ibig pong sabihin yung utilization ng 2026.

**[02:03:23.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001454)  
Ibig pong sabihin yung utilization ng 2026 ay mas mataas kaysa sa 2025.

**[02:03:27.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001455)  
Tama po ba yan?

**[02:03:28.300] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001456)  
82% ang utilization.

**[02:03:52.150] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001457)  
Tama po yan.

**[02:03:53.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001460)  
Ang base po sa datos ng DBM, mas mataas po yung utilization rate ng 2026 ng LGSF

**[02:03:59.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001461)  
kumpara po sa 2025 ng LGSF.

**[02:04:01.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001462)  
Ang kadahilanan po ay yung LGSF price.

**[02:04:05.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001463)  
Yun po yung mataas yung utilization rate.

**[02:04:07.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001464)  
Meron po siya sa 2026, pero wala po siya sa 2025.

**[02:04:13.120] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001465)  
Ibig pong sabihin, mas madaling na nagamit yung pondo ng 2026.

**[02:04:20.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001466)  
Bakit po kaya madaling na gamit yung price?

**[02:04:22.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001467)  
Bakit po kaya madaling na gamit yung price?

**[02:04:23.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001468)  
Binamigay na lang lahat sa lahat yung rice.

**[02:04:27.220] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001469)  
Ano po ang development economic impact nung pagbibigay ng bigas ng kanyang kalalaki?

**[02:04:44.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001470)  
Mabilis po kasi yung distribution ng rice kasi po lahat po ng mga LGUs sa buong bansa nabigyan po.

**[02:04:50.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001471)  
So mas mabilis po siya kumpara sa halimbawa.

**[02:04:53.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001472)  
Halimbawa yung individual projects na rinirequest po ng LGU doon sa portal.

**[02:04:58.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001473)  
Yung dahilan po kung bakit ipinamigay po yung bigas ay bilang pagresponde po doon sa iba't ibang mga krisis.

**[02:05:08.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001474)  
Halimbawa po yung pagtaas po ng presyo ng crudo, ng koryente, at yung mga nawalan din po ng trabaho.

**[02:05:17.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001475)  
Kaya kailangan po matugunan din po kagad.

**[02:05:18.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001476)  
Talagang ayuda rin po ito.

**[02:05:22.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001477)  
Ayuda to, di ba?

**[02:05:24.020] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001478)  
Walang economic impact to eh.

**[02:05:25.480] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001479)  
Kundi pagbibigay ng bigas, limang kilo, sampung kilo.

**[02:05:30.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001480)  
Hindi ba ito patronage politics?

**[02:05:36.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001481)  
There is an economic factor din po which is where we are endeavoring po na hindi po bumagsak sa below poverty line.

**[02:05:46.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001482)  
Yung mga pamilya na pinaka-vulnerable po dito po sa mga ganitong external shock.

**[02:05:53.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001483)  
So there's also a multiplier effect in the sense that yung mga bigas po ay kinukuha sa mga local farmers.

**[02:06:03.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001484)  
So meron po din guaranteed na bumibili noong bigas, yung mga palay, yung mga produkto na ating mga magsasaka.

**[02:06:10.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001485)  
So meron din po siyang economic, may economic ecosystem din po.

**[02:06:17.240] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001486)  
Namomonitor po ba ng PBM yung pagbibigay ng bigas?

**[02:06:23.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001487)  
Ang DILG po, ang nag-incharge po ng monitoring ng distribution ng LGS price.

**[02:06:31.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001488)  
Bibigyan ko po kayo ng isang example sa Kaloocan.

**[02:06:35.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001489)  
Yung isang banko, Philippine Business Bank.

**[02:06:37.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001490)  
Lahat na empleyado, from manager to ordinary employee, tigko 40 kilos na bigas.

**[02:06:46.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001491)  
40 kilos.

**[02:06:48.460] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001492)  
So exclusively at the discretion.

**[02:06:53.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001493)  
Paano nakakatulong ito sa mga mahirap kung ang binibigyan mo ang mga executive ng banko?

**[02:07:04.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001494)  
Madam Speaker, given that the DILG is in charge of the monitoring, yun po dapat yung mga bagay na huhuli ng DILG.

**[02:07:15.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001495)  
So kung may mga ganito pong pagkakataon, dapat po ma-report po sa DILG para po matugunan nila.

**[02:07:22.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001496)  
So meron po ba?

**[02:07:23.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001497)  
Ito ang assessment na nagawa ang PBM sa utilization ng 2025 at least.

**[02:07:30.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001498)  
2025 LGSF.

**[02:07:32.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001499)  
And can you furnish us a copy?

**[02:07:34.460] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001500)  
Thank you.

**[02:07:53.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001501)  
Madam Speaker, may I ask po our distinguished colleague, what sort of report po would you like for the 2025 utilization?

**[02:08:11.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001502)  
How it was distributed?

**[02:08:12.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001503)  
How it was distributed?

**[02:08:13.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001504)  
In terms of which LGUs po?

**[02:08:17.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001506)  
Are the recipients the types of projects?

**[02:08:19.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001507)  
Yes.

**[02:08:20.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001508)  
Apo.

**[02:08:20.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001509)  
Sa kasalukuyan po, yung...

**[02:08:23.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001510)  
So far po, yung roughly 52% na na-dispersed po for 2025, lahat po ng datos doon po sa releases, nandun na rin po, naka-upload na po sa Compass portal ng DBM.

**[02:08:35.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001511)  
Pero magbibigay din po.

**[02:08:36.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001512)  
Ang nakalagay po doon yung amount of releases o yung goods that were given?

**[02:08:45.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001513)  
Nandito po sa Compass, actually po, nakita ko po ngayon ang ganda po ng dashboard nila.

**[02:08:51.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001514)  
And nandun po yung...

**[02:08:53.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001515)  
Bawat proyekto at bawat LGU at magkano at kung anong klaseng proyekto po.

**[02:09:00.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001516)  
Halimbawa po, NCR, Metro Manila, Manila, Multipurpose Building, Construction of Pamantasan ng Lungsod na Maynila, College of Allied Health Sciences, 500 million, year 2025 po.

**[02:09:15.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001517)  
So pwede nating ma-filter po yun doon po sa Compass website.

**[02:09:18.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001518)  
Sa apalagay niyo po, Madam Chair, equitable po ang distribution nito?

**[02:09:23.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001519)  
Equitable.

**[02:09:36.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001520)  
For 2026 po, Madam Speaker, equitable po dahil po lahat binigyan.

**[02:09:44.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001521)  
Yung 2025 po, nakabatay po kasi sa kung sino yung nag-request.

**[02:09:48.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001522)  
So posible po na may ibang mga LGU, may ibang mga probinsya.

**[02:09:53.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001523)  
Na hindi po nag-request. So wala po silang natanggap.

**[02:09:56.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001524)  
So sa 2027 po, paano po i-dedistribute ito?

**[02:10:16.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001525)  
Para po sa taong 2027, dahil po yung panukalang amount po ng LGU,

**[02:10:23.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001526)  
GSF ay parehas doon sa 2026 ga.

**[02:10:27.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001527)  
Ang intensyon po ayon sa DBM ay pareho pa rin po yung distribution.

**[02:10:32.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001528)  
So with regard to rice, lahat pa rin po na local government units mabibigyan.

**[02:10:37.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001529)  
And with regard to infrastructure po, it would still depend on whether there's an LGU that requested from a particular area.

**[02:10:46.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001530)  
And it would still be consistent po with the same menu and criteria that we approve.

**[02:10:52.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001531)  
So in the 2027 ga, under the special provision for LGSF.

**[02:10:57.060] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001532)  
With the indulgence of our good mentor.

**[02:10:59.480] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001533)  
Last question, ma'am. Thank you.

**[02:11:04.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001534)  
Kaya po ako nagtatanong, sapagkat napakamahalaga po na masinop yung paggamit natin ng 7.2 trillion.

**[02:11:13.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001535)  
Sapagkat sa 17.2 trillion, ang projected government revenue ay 5.2 trillion lamang. Tama po?

**[02:11:22.220] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001536)  
At yun ay projected pa lang.

**[02:11:26.420] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001537)  
Sa bagsak nating GDP, baka hindi pa ma-realize.

**[02:11:31.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001538)  
Pagkatapos po, ang uutangin natin para ponduhan nito ay 1.69 trillion.

**[02:11:38.620] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001539)  
Sa 1.69 trillion po, for development projects, kasama na yung mga ayuda, 1.1 trillion.

**[02:11:46.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001540)  
Ibig sabihin, yung napuponduhan ng pagutang natin, pati yung personal services,

**[02:11:52.220] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001541)  
pati yung MOOE. Kaya po mahalaga.

**[02:11:56.280] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001542)  
At pagkatapos po, kailangan pa nating mangutang muli ng 3.3 trillion para bayaran yung mga nag-mature na utang natin.

**[02:12:07.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001543)  
Tama po ba yan?

**[02:12:10.420] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001544)  
Aside from the 1.69 trillion na uutangin para ponduhan ng 2027 national budget,

para ipatigil ang expenditure

for a project?

**[02:29:55.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001770)  
Change of policy?

**[02:29:57.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001771)  
Change of priority?

**[02:29:57.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001772)  
May problema?

**[02:30:00.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001773)  
Ano malya? Ano ba ang objective standard natin?

**[02:30:04.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001774)  
And has this provision been invoked already in 2026?

**[02:30:10.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001775)  
Madam Speaker, there is actually jurisprudence with regard to this matter.

**[02:30:15.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001776)  
The Supreme Court has already ruled that the definition of when public interest requires is quite vague.

**[02:30:26.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001777)  
In essence, leaving it up to the President to decide on that.

**[02:30:32.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001778)  
But we're pulling right now, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, this specific jurisprudence so we can cite it correctly.

**[02:30:42.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001779)  
Yung Arawlio po. Arawlio. You can look at Arawlio.

**[02:30:53.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001780)  
Madam Speaker, the DBM is just pulling.

**[02:30:56.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001781)  
The DBM is just pulling up the specific jurisprudence.

**[02:31:00.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001782)  
Madam Chair, Distinguished Colleague, has this been invoked in 2026?

**[02:31:08.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001783)  
Madam Speaker, no. This particular general provision has not been invoked.

**[02:31:16.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001784)  
Diretso ko na lang po dun sa susunod kong tanong.

**[02:31:22.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001785)  
Madam Chair, under the new Section 78,

**[02:31:26.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001786)  
pwede ba ninyong gamitin ang public interest para hindi na i-release ang allotment for a project in the first place?

**[02:31:34.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001787)  
Yes or no?

**[02:31:56.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001788)  
Madam Speaker, the answer is no po, given that these speak of two different things.

**[02:32:07.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001789)  
Releases is different from suspension.

**[02:32:09.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001790)  
So this particular general provision only deals with suspension.

**[02:32:14.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001791)  
So hindi po pwedeng gamitin yung general provision na ito para hindi po i-release ang isa pong allocation or funding.

**[02:32:23.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001792)  
Then Madam Chair, can DBM kadilis?

**[02:32:26.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001793)  
DBM kategorikali confirm that public interest under Section 78 cannot be used to withhold the initial release of an allotment.

**[02:32:51.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001794)  
Madam Speaker, yes, the category...

**[02:32:54.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001795)  
My apologies, yes and no.

**[02:32:55.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001796)  
Medyo confusing po.

**[02:32:56.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001797)  
The categorical answer of DBM is no.

**[02:33:01.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001798)  
Hindi po pwedeng gamitin ito para mag-hold ng releases.

**[02:33:05.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001799)  
Then Madam Chair, Distinguished Colleague,

**[02:33:07.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001800)  
what provision now protects appropriations against impoundment

**[02:33:13.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001801)  
considering that the express prohibition against impoundment provision was removed?

**[02:33:56.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001807)  
May time?

**[02:33:57.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001808)  
Please suspend.

**[02:33:58.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001809)  
Thank you.

**[02:33:59.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001810)  
Thank you.

**[02:34:20.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001811)  
Madam Speaker, if I may.

**[02:34:23.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001812)  
As a response, there is general provision number 3.

**[02:34:27.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001813)  
Which pertains to the General Appropriations Act as allotment order.

**[02:34:32.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001814)  
It says here that the General Appropriations Act upon its effectivity shall be considered the allotment authorizing agencies to obligate in accordance with its provisions except for two conditions.

**[02:34:43.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001815)  
The first would be the appropriations that by virtue of law, general or special provisions, and rules and regulations, have conditions or requirements before release, and lump sum appropriations in the agency budget.

**[02:34:56.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001816)  
And special purpose funds that have no details necessary for release. So yun po yung safeguard, General Provision No. 3 po.

**[02:35:03.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001817)  
Can you please

**[02:35:06.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001818)  
So there are specific conditions by which a particular allotment would not be released; otherwise the general rule is it should be released.

**[02:35:17.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001819)  
Bakit po yung ano? Bakit po yung based doon sa NEP?

**[02:35:21.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001820)  
Bakit po yung congressional initiatives

**[02:35:24.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001821)  
Bakit po?

**[02:35:26.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001826)  
Bakit po yung congressional initiatives, nirequire ng aficero tapos nag-require ng additional requirements?

**[02:35:42.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001827)  
Wala po yun sa binasa nyo, Madam Chair.

**[02:35:46.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001828)  
In the case po, I wouldn't call them congressional initiatives.

**[02:35:50.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001829)  
Those are probably, with your permission po, we'll tag them as items that have deviations between the NEP and the GAA.

**[02:35:58.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001830)  
So, yun po kasi, those are items that have not been vetted by the DBM.

**[02:36:04.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001831)  
It didn't go through the usual process that the other projects had to undergo during the budget call.

**[02:36:12.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001832)  
So, in that case po, it's an additional safeguard on the part of DBM

**[02:36:17.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001833)  
to be able to make sure that these projects,

**[02:36:19.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001834)  
comply with the same requirements as the projects in the NEP.

**[02:36:23.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001835)  
So, meron pong ganong additional safeguard, kaya po naging FISARO po.

**[02:36:28.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001836)  
Ano po yung legal basis para gawin yun?

**[02:36:34.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001837)  
Every congressional initiative or amendment was done, dumaan po yan sa RDC and was properly vetted.

**[02:36:52.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001839)  
I wanted to know what the legal basis was for the president to do a veto.

**[02:36:55.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001840)  
Are you saying, Madam Chair,

**[02:36:58.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001841)  
distinguished colleague, na pag dumaan po sa atin,

**[02:37:01.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001842)  
dumaan po sa congressional,

**[02:37:04.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001843)  
sa House, dumaan sa Senate, hindi po natin binibeta?

**[02:37:07.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001844)  
The legal basis po for this, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague,

**[02:37:10.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001845)  
is the veto power of the president as indicated in the veto message.

**[02:37:16.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001846)  
There's a particular section there in the veto message that

**[02:37:19.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001847)  
that pertains to increases in appropriations and new budgetary items.

**[02:37:23.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001848)  
At doon po, linae out po yung mga conditions na kailangan po ma-meet for the release of these items

**[02:37:32.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001849)  
that are either new budgetary items or saw increases in appropriations.

**[02:37:38.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001850)  
In the 2026 GAA, ano yung veto message?

**[02:37:45.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001851)  
Saan po nakalagay na veto message yun?

**[02:37:47.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001852)  
Wala po sa veto message yun.

**[02:37:51.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001853)  
Doon po sa mismong veto message, it is under Section 6 with the header

**[02:37:57.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001854)  
Increases in Appropriations and New Budgetary Items as Published po.

**[02:38:02.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001855)  
With the veto message as published in the official gazette.

**[02:38:05.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001856)  
Under what provision po nakalagay sa veto message?

**[02:38:09.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001857)  
Item 6 po.

**[02:38:11.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001858)  
What does it state specifically?

**[02:38:12.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001859)  
It pertains to increases in appropriations and new budgetary items.

**[02:38:17.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001860)  
I could read po the particular item if our distinguished colleague so desires.

**[02:38:27.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001861)  
In the exercise of the budget execution function vested upon the executive branch,

**[02:38:31.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001862)  
it is understood that the increases in appropriations and new budgetary items introduced by the Congress in this budget

**[02:38:37.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001863)  
shall be subject to the national government's cash programming,

**[02:38:40.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001864)  
observance of prudent fiscal management,

**[02:38:44.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001865)  
applicable budget execution rules and procedures,

**[02:38:46.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001866)  
and,

**[02:38:47.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001867)  
and approval by the president based on the program priorities of the government.

**[02:38:51.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001868)  
Considering that such releases in appropriations and new budgetary items will have corresponding effects

**[02:38:56.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001869)  
on the outputs and outcomes of the agencies concerned,

**[02:38:59.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001870)  
the DBM shall inform the said agencies of the changes in their respective appropriations

**[02:39:03.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001871)  
and require the submission of their revised performance target.

**[02:39:07.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001872)  
So nasa first paragraph po, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues, yung mga conditions.

**[02:39:13.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001873)  
Thank you for that, Madam Chair, distinguished colleague.

**[02:39:16.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001874)  
Madam Chair,

**[02:39:17.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001875)  
may I apply natin kung ang isang project ay nasa FISARO at wala pang SARO,

**[02:39:23.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001876)  
can public interest be invoked so that the SARO is never released?

**[02:39:28.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001877)  
Yes or no?

**[02:39:33.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001878)  
My apologies, Madam Chair, Madam Speaker, medyo technical po kasi.

**[02:39:38.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001879)  
May I request for the question to be reiterated, releases po ba or suspension?

**[02:39:43.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001880)  
No, I'll just...

**[02:39:47.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001881)  
I'll just repeat the question.

**[02:39:48.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001882)  
Yes, please po.

**[02:39:48.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001883)  
Kung ang isang project po ay nasa FISARO at wala pang SARO,

**[02:39:53.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001884)  
can public interest be invoked so that the SARO is never released?

**[02:40:14.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001903)  
Madam Speaker, given that the question pertains to releases of items categorized under FISARO, opo, sa ilalim po nitong Section 6 ng veto message, maaari pong mangyari yan.

**[02:40:23.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001920)  
Opo.

**[02:40:24.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001921)  
Sa ilalim po nitong section six ng veto message, maaari pong mangyari yan if several of the conditions, one of the conditions set forth are met.

**[02:40:36.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001922)  
Either...

**[02:40:37.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001923)  
Hindi...

**[02:40:38.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001924)  
It could either not meet the national government's cash programming or prudent fiscal management,

**[02:40:46.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001925)  
or it went against applicable budget execution rules and procedures,

**[02:40:52.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001926)  
if any of the conditions are not met, it is possible po that an item under PESARO will not be released under Section 6 of the veto message.

**[02:41:02.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001927)  
How about, Madam Chair, distinguished colleagues, how about appropriations or line items introduced or amended by Congress?

**[02:41:12.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001928)  
Can the Executive likewise refuse the release by invoking public interest?

**[02:41:21.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001929)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. Given that whichever was included in either the HGAB or the SGAB or the BICAM that was not there in the NEPFOS under new budgetary items or increases in appropriations, the same section holds po. So it is possible.

**[02:41:39.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001930)  
So, kung hindi po natin tatanggal,

**[02:41:42.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001931)  
kung hindi po natin tatanggalin yung provision, which okay naman na sinasabi ng DBM, willing silang tanggalin yung provision on suspension.

**[02:41:51.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001932)  
For 2026, bakit hindi po kasi, bakit hindi na lang veto ang nangyari?

**[02:41:59.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001933)  
Kasi parang nakakatakot po eh. Kung pagkatapos ba in-act ang GAA, pwede nang piliin ng Executive.

**[02:42:05.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001934)  
Given this provision, pwede na pong piliin ng Executive kung alin ang ilalabas at alin ang hindi.

**[02:42:12.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001935)  
Hindi simply ang hindi i-invoke at alin ang hindi simply by invoking public interest, hindi pa parang nire-rewrite na ng Executive ang GAA.

**[02:42:42.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001936)  
Madam Speaker, ang nangyari po kasi kung dumedetsa po kaagad sa veto, given the volume of the line items of the budget that would be affected by the condition,

**[02:43:06.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001937)  
which would be the increases in appropriations or the new budgetary items,

**[02:43:12.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001938)  
maapektuhan po kasi yung essentially marami pong mga projects and programs and activities na madi-disallow kung dalesyo po tayo sa veto.

**[02:43:22.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001939)  
So ang ginawa nilang po as a safeguard, sinubject nilang po doon sa iba't ibang mga kondisyon.

**[02:43:28.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001940)  
So if straight to veto po kasi, ang mangyayari matatanggal po lahat sila so that would affect economic growth and government expenditure.

**[02:43:38.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001941)  
Hindi po ba pwede, Madam Chair, na very...

**[02:43:42.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001942)  
Like you mentioned, very specific naman yung veto message.

**[02:43:46.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001943)  
It can be very specific where kung ano yung ayaw ng Presidente na provision, yun lang ang i-veto niya.

**[02:43:59.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001944)  
And may I ask, bakit nyo po sinasabing maapektuhan ng economic growth?

**[02:44:05.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001945)  
Kasi parang ang sinasabi nyo, effectively, by saying that this will affect economic growth,

**[02:44:12.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001946)  
hindi ba nyo sinasabi na parang gagamitin natin ito as savings?

**[02:44:17.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001947)  
Madam Speaker, Mr. Speaker, actually po yun po yung ginawa ng Presidente.

**[02:44:23.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001948)  
Nag-line veto lang po siya ng particular items doon po sa 2026 budget.

**[02:44:29.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001949)  
But ito po is the veto message, the Section 6 of veto message specifically governs increases in appropriations and new budgetary items.

**[02:44:40.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001950)  
Based on your answer lang, Madam Chair.

**[02:44:42.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001951)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Presidente.

**[02:44:44.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001952)  
Distinguished colleague, parang ang sinasabi nyo kasi, the reason why we introduced this provision is for the purpose na magkaroon ng flexibility yung Presidente para kung sakaling gagamitin niya, i-invoke niya yung public interest.

**[02:45:00.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001953)  
interest na

**[02:45:01.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001954)  
provision, ay pwede pong

**[02:45:03.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001955)  
gamitin yung pera

**[02:45:06.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001956)  
at ilipat sa ibang

**[02:45:07.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001957)  
mga proyekto.

**[02:45:15.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001958)  
May time, please.

**[02:45:18.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001959)  
Pakitigil.

**[02:46:43.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001964)  
Mr. Speaker, actually po kahit wala po itong provision na 'to, kahit wala po itong general provision na 'to, the President has the power vested by the Constitution to use savings.

**[02:47:00.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001965)  
Under Section 25,

**[02:47:02.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001966)  
Subsection 5,

**[02:47:04.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001967)  
Article 6 of the

**[02:47:05.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001968)  
1987 Constitution,

**[02:47:08.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001969)  
no law shall be passed

**[02:47:09.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001970)  
authorizing any transfer

**[02:47:11.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001971)  
of appropriations. However,

**[02:47:13.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001972)  
the President, the President of the Senate,

**[02:47:15.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001973)  
the Speaker of the House of Representatives, the Chief

**[02:47:17.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001974)  
Justice of the Supreme Court, and the

**[02:47:19.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001975)  
Heads of Constitutional Commissions

**[02:47:21.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001976)  
may, by law, be authorized

**[02:47:23.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001977)  
to augment any item in the General

**[02:47:25.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001978)  
Appropriations Law for their respective

**[02:47:27.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001979)  
offices from savings in other

**[02:47:29.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001980)  
items of their respective

**[02:47:31.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001981)  
appropriations. So, mayroon din pong

**[02:47:33.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001982)  
constitutional basis ito pong

**[02:47:35.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001983)  
use of savings ng Pangulo.

**[02:47:39.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001984)  
Sige. Madam Chair,

**[02:47:41.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001985)  
kapag, I'll just go

**[02:47:43.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001986)  
on to my next question, no?

**[02:47:45.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001987)  
Diyan rin naman tayo pupunta.

**[02:47:47.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001988)  
Kapag pinatigil ang isang project under

**[02:47:49.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001989)  
Section 78, is it

**[02:47:51.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001990)  
merely suspended

**[02:47:52.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001991)  
or is it finally

**[02:47:54.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001992)  
discontinued or abandoned?

**[02:47:56.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001993)  
Is it finally discontinued?

**[02:47:59.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001994)  
Madam Speaker,

**[03:00:14.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002201)  
Mr. Speaker, if I may respond to our distinguished colleague.

**[03:00:16.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002202)  
So looking at the Araulio jurisprudence, it defines the terminology that would allow for the use of savings.

**[03:00:27.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002203)  
If I may read out, just if you would allow me, Section 60, Section 54, and Section 53 of the General Provisions of the 2011, 2012, and 2013 GAAS, respectively, contemplate three sources of savings.

**[03:00:45.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002204)  
So dinidefine po yung tatlong sources ng savings.

**[03:00:49.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002205)  
First, there can be savings when there are funds still available after completion of work, activity, or project,

**[03:00:56.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002206)  
which means there are excess funds remaining after the work, activity, or project is completed.

**[03:01:02.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002207)  
There can also be savings when there is final discontinuance of the work, activity, or project,

**[03:01:08.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002208)  
which means that there are funds remaining after work, activity, or project was started but finally discontinued before completion.

**[03:01:18.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002209)  
To illustrate, a bridge halfway completed is destroyed by floods or earthquake and thus finally discontinued

**[03:01:24.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002210)  
because the remaining funds are not sufficient.

**[03:01:26.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002211)  
Here, the funds are obligated but the remaining funds are deobligated upon final discontinuance of the project.

**[03:01:35.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002212)  
On the other hand, abandonment means the work, activity, or project can no longer be started because of lack of time to obligate the funds,

**[03:01:44.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002213)  
resulting in the physical impossibility to obligate the funds.

**[03:01:48.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002214)  
This happens when a month or two before the end of the fiscal year, there is no more time to conduct a public bidding to obligate the funds.

**[03:01:55.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002218)  
The funds are not and can no longer be obligated and thus will constitute savings.

**[03:02:01.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002219)  
Dito po tayo sa last sentence mag-align.

**[03:02:05.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002220)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, final discontinuance or abandonment excludes suspension or temporary stoppage of the work, activity, or the project.

**[03:02:17.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002221)  
Suspension, as it is referred to here in the Arroyo jurisprudence,

**[03:02:25.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002223)  
is the whole phrase po, suspension or temporary stoppage of the work, activity, or project.

**[03:02:31.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002224)  
So it is not the same suspension that is talked about in Section 78 of the General Provision.

**[03:02:41.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002225)  
Hindi po yun ang tinatanong ko, Madam Chair.

**[03:02:45.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002226)

**[03:02:54.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002227)  
even before the end of the fiscal year.

**[03:02:55.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002228)  
Even before the end of the fiscal year.

**[03:02:55.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002229)  
Even before allotment.

**[03:02:57.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002230)

**[03:03:08.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002231)

**[03:03:16.660] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002232)  
The Chair would like to remind Honorable Nazal, your time has expired.

**[03:03:21.640] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002233)

**[03:03:25.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002234)
