**[03:06:48.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002240)  
Mr. Speaker, we found the paragraph that Representative Nazal was referring to. If I may draw attention to a particular line which says Section 38 cannot be used by the President to stop permanently the expenditure of unobligated appropriated funds because that would amount to presidential power to impound funds appropriated in the GAA.

**[03:06:55.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002241)  
At yung nire-refer po ni Cong Robert is the next line: the President has no power to impound unobligated funds in the GAA. The operative word here po would be unobligated funds, and if something is categorized as unobligated, ibig sabihin po na-release na po iyon.

**[03:07:25.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002242)  
So it's different from something that has not been released. So ibig sabihin, the operative term here is that the President has no power to impound funds in the GAA that have been released but not yet obligated.

**[03:07:55.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002243)  
That's why in the succeeding sentences in the same paragraph the ruling reiterated the two reasons as also outlined in Section 84 on declaration of savings. So yun po, siguro po yung key doon: unobligated means it has already been released.

**[03:08:25.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002244)  
So kailangan may SARO na. Kailangan po may SARO na.

**[03:08:28.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002244-split-2)

**[03:08:34.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002244-split-3)  
Yes, yung use of savings po. The suspension applies to

**[03:08:42.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002244-split-4)

**[03:08:46.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002244-split-5)

**[03:08:55.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002245)

**[03:09:01.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002245-split-2)

**[03:09:15.000] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002245-split-3)  
All right. Honorable Nazal, your time is up.

Final from the Chair.

from 2016 to 20...

Okay, proposed.

**[03:37:16.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002943)  
Well, what is remarkable here?

**[03:37:18.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002944)

**[03:37:20.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002945)  
Actually, you will see that una muna,

**[03:37:25.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002946)  
from 2016 to 2021,

**[03:37:29.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002947)

**[03:37:32.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002948)

**[03:37:35.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002949)  
yun din yung aprobahan ng Kongreso.

**[03:37:38.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002950)

**[03:37:41.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002951)  
No?

**[03:37:42.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002952)  
2016, 67.5 billion yung UA.

**[03:37:46.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002953)  
And then 2021, kasagsagan ng pandemia,

**[03:37:51.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002954)  
unprogrammed is 176 billion.

**[03:37:56.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002955)  
Kung anong pinroposed ng NEP, yun din ang in-approve ng Kongreso.

**[03:37:59.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002956)  
Pero, starting in 2022, the last year,

**[03:38:03.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002957)  
the last budget of the Duterte administration,

**[03:38:06.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002958)  
ito yung unang kagat sa mansanas.

**[03:38:09.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002959)  
Ika nga. No?

**[03:38:10.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002960)  
Biglang natuklasan ng Kongreso.

**[03:38:12.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002961)  
Teka muna.

**[03:38:13.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002962)  
Pwede pala nating lakihan tong unprogrammed appropriations.

**[03:38:17.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002963)  
So, 2022, the proposed is 151B.

**[03:38:22.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002964)  
But GAA, 251B.

**[03:38:26.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002965)  
So, lumaki by 100B.

**[03:38:29.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002966)  
And then, really, pagupo ng Marcos Jr. administration, tinodo na.

**[03:38:36.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002967)  
No?

**[03:38:37.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002968)  
Yung pag-abuso sa unprogrammed appropriations.

**[03:38:39.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002969)  
2023, unbelievable, 807 billion ang unprogrammed appropriations.

**[03:38:49.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002970)  
Tapos 2024, 731 billion.

**[03:38:54.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002971)  
And then 2025, bumaba sa 336.

**[03:38:59.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002972)  
Tapos dahil naging constitutional issue na ito, and so on and so forth,

**[03:39:04.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002973)  
ayan, papaliit ngayon ang unprogrammed.

**[03:39:07.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002974)  
Ang iba nga. Sinasabi.

**[03:39:08.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002975)  
Dapat tanggalin na outright.

**[03:39:10.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002976)  
Okay.

**[03:39:11.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002977)  
But this is the story of unprogrammed appropriations.

**[03:39:14.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002978)  
No?

**[03:39:15.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002979)  
Now, why is this relevant?

**[03:39:18.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002980)  
No?

**[03:39:19.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002981)  
Ano ba ang nangyari?

**[03:39:20.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002982)  
Well, alam natin na yung modus ng unprogrammed appropriations, bakit lumaki ng ganyan?

**[03:39:27.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002983)  
Because regular items of the budget were being forced into unprogrammed appropriations

**[03:39:35.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002984)  
to create space for...

**[03:39:38.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002985)  
Congressional insertions.

**[03:39:39.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002986)  
Yung mga dagdag na ayuda, dagdag na flood control, at iba pa.

**[03:39:45.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002987)  
No?

**[03:39:46.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002988)  
Tama po ba?

**[03:39:48.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002989)  
Are my facts correct?

**[03:39:51.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002990)  
Madam sponsor, tama po ba yan?

**[03:39:56.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002991)  
Mr. Speaker, siguro po ang aking pong nais-isagot sa ating kasamahan.

**[03:40:04.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002993)  
Yung 2026 po.

**[03:40:05.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002994)  
Siguro doon po kasi ako nagsimula man nung...

**[03:40:07.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002996)  
Nagsimula man nung kulan bilang chairperson ng Committee on Appropriations.

**[03:40:12.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002999)  
Yun po yung maaari ko pong maipaliwanag.

**[03:40:14.790] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003001)  
Because that is within my purview na po.

**[03:40:17.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003002)  
So ang ginawa po talaga natin noong 2026, sinagurado po natin, nalinagyan po natin ng

**[03:40:22.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003003)  
karampatang safeguards yung mga unprogrammed appropriations po.

**[03:40:26.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003004)  
Makikita po sa version po ng house na inaproobahan po natin ng GAB, tinanggal nga po natin

**[03:40:33.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003005)  
yung infrastructure mula doon sa sagip.

**[03:40:35.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003006)  
So hindi na po natin pinayagan na magkaroon po ng kahit ano na releases patungo sa infrastructure

**[03:40:43.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003007)  
galing po sa unprogrammed appropriations.

**[03:40:45.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003008)  
Kaya makikita din po natin yung ating ginawang pag-rationalize ng mga allowable items under

**[03:40:52.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003009)  
unprogrammed appropriations during the HGAB as well as yung veto po na isinagawa na ating

**[03:40:58.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003010)  
Pangulo sa 2026 GAA nung unprogrammed appropriations.

**[03:41:03.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003011)  
Nakita po natin ang laki po ng binaba.

**[03:41:04.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003012)  
At ngayon po ang 2027, kung titignan po natin, ito po yung pinakamababa.

**[03:41:11.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003013)  
In nominal terms po, yung unprogrammed appropriations for the year 2027 as proposed in the NEP

**[03:41:19.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003014)  
is the lowest since 1991 as a percentage of the budget.

**[03:41:25.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003015)  
Kung nominal terms naman po yung ating titignan, siguro po pagbabasihan po natin dito, at least 2019 po,

**[03:41:32.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003016)  
yun po yung pinakamababa in nominal terms.

**[03:41:35.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003017)  
So, rest assured po, in the 2027 budget, sisiguraduhin po natin na kung ano man yung mga papayagan natin,

**[03:41:44.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003018)  
yung mga allowable uses under the unprogrammed appropriations na ating po aaprobahan sa House version ng General Appropriations Bill,

**[03:41:53.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003019)  
sisiguraduhin po talaga natin na may safeguards yun at talagang doon lang pupunta sa pinakakinakailangan.

**[03:42:00.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003020)  
Halimbawa po yung mga foreign assisted projects, risk management, yung mga obligasyon po natin na kinakailangan-kailangan po talaga ng standby funds.

**[03:42:10.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003021)  
Okay, thank you Madam Chair. So, ang point ko dito sa overview, baliktanaw sa 10 years ng pagbabudget,

**[03:42:18.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003022)  
una, na markado talaga ang paglaki ng utang starting during the pandemic.

**[03:42:25.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003023)  
Naintindihan pa natin siguro, kailangan talagang umutang.

**[03:42:28.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003024)  
Pero nagtuloy-tuloy ito. Ngayon lumaki na rin yung deficit spending sa panahon yun.

**[03:42:37.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003025)  
So, ang tanong ngayon, saan ginamit yung utang? Napakinabangan ba ng taong bayan?

**[03:42:45.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003026)  
Pinakita natin, well, malaking bahagi ng inutang ginamit para mapalaki yung DPWH budget.

**[03:42:56.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003027)  
Particularly flood control. Flood control lang pinakita natin dito. Hindi natin sinama yung mga iba pa, mga road, bridges, etc.

**[03:43:04.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003028)  
But it follows the same trend. So, saan ginamit ang utang? Ginamit ang utang para sa flood control.

**[03:43:13.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003029)  
Ginamit din ang utang para palakihin astronomically ang ayuda.

**[03:43:21.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003030)  
So, yung TUPAD, yung AICS.

**[03:43:26.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003031)  
So, anong naging pakinabang ng sambayan ng Pilipino?

**[03:43:34.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003032)  
At ano ang naging pakinabang sa ekonomiya, sa pambansa, sa pag-unlad?

**[03:43:39.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003033)  
Ito na po ang point natin. Dahil sa ganito, ang nangyari, alam natin nangyari sa flood control.

**[03:43:47.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003034)  
Ang nangyari sa flood control, ang laki ng binuhos, lumalala pa ang baha.

**[03:43:53.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003035)  
Samantala, ang daming, ayan na, naging bahagi na ng araw-araw na pananalita ng mga kababayan natin, yung maleta.

**[03:44:04.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003036)  
Ayan na, ang daming nakatanggap daw ng maleta. That's because of the flood control corruption scandal, plunder.

**[03:44:13.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003037)  
So, a significant portion of the flood control money, according to whistleblowers,

**[03:44:22.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003038)  
like Bernardo, Yusec Bernardo, former Secretary Bonoan, napunta raw sa kickback.

**[03:44:28.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003039)  
Diba? As high as 30 to 50 percent of that money.

**[03:44:33.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003040)  
Okay. So, doon napunta sa corruption. Hindi sa aktual na proyektong pinakinabangan ng taong bayan.

**[03:44:39.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003041)  
Ganun din po. Paano naman yung ayuda?

**[03:44:42.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003042)  
Sabi ko nga, TUPAD. Sorry, AICS.

**[03:44:47.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003043)  
9,000 percent growth in AICS.

**[03:44:51.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003044)  
Growth in AICS over a decade. Okay. Sabihin, well, natulungan naman ang kababayan natin ng AICS. And it's true.

**[03:45:00.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003045)  
Diba? For the most part, talaga namang nakakatulong sa taong bag. Ganun din naman yung MyFIP. Diba? It's a medical assistance. Sure. Okay. Pero ang sinasabi nga, ito yung patronage-based. Kailangan dumaan ka kay congressman, kailangan dumaan ka kay senator o kung sino paman. Okay.

**[03:45:22.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003046)  
The other question is, so napunta, in other words, saan napunta ang napakalaking inutang?

**[03:45:28.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003047)  
Ang pinupunta po natin dito ay napunta sa pork barrel, flood control, at sa patronage.

**[03:45:37.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003048)  
Social services as patronage. Okay.

**[03:45:41.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003049)  
Saan hindi napunta?

**[03:45:43.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003050)  
Hindi napunta, I mean, napakalaking ng inutang natin.

**[03:45:48.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003051)  
Pwede rin naman sana inilagay ito.

**[03:45:51.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003052)  
Sa pagpapalakas ng agrikultura, pagpapalakas ng manupaktura.

**[03:45:58.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003053)  
Nakikita ko si Dep Dev Sek Balisakan.

**[03:46:03.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003054)  
Sinasabi niya, ang problema raw ng ekonomiya natin, largely consumption-driven.

**[03:46:09.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003055)  
Well, pagka puro ayuda ang binibigay mo, ayun, consumption-driven.

**[03:46:14.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003056)  
Hindi ka lumili ka ng bago at pangmatagalang trabaho.

**[03:46:20.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003057)  
So, Madam, Mr. Chair, Madam Speaker, marami pa po tayo. Parang part one pa lang po tayo.

**[03:46:31.880] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-003058)  
The Chair would like to remind Honorable Tinio that your time has expired.

**[03:46:36.840] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-003059)  
Okay. So, the Honorable Suansing will respond.

**[03:46:41.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003060)  
Yes.

**[03:46:42.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003061)  
No. Mr. Speaker, that was just my first point, no, that nabaon po tayo sa utang.

**[03:46:50.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003062)  
Okay. Especially under the Marcos Jr. Administration, where did that debt go?

**[03:46:56.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003063)  
No, it went to fund pork barrel, yung flood control, at saka yung pagpapalaki ng ayuda.

**[03:47:07.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003064)  
No. So, dependence instead of economic independence.

**[03:47:14.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003065)  
Mr. Speaker, marami pa po tayong tanong. I suppose I can continue this.

**[03:47:20.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003066)  
During the interpellation of DEVDEV and then DBM, since magkakaugnay naman po yung mga tanong natin.

**[03:47:28.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003067)  
So, hanggang dyan na lang po. Marami kalamat.

**[03:47:30.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003068)  
Thank you. Majority Leader.

**[03:47:31.580] Floor Leader** (asr-003069)

**[04:42:38.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003800)  
they have several privatization projects in the pipeline that would, we'll give the example.

**[04:42:48.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003801)  
We can mention several po of these privatization projects in the pipeline.

**[04:42:55.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003802)  
So there's the Mile Long asset, FTI, SLEX shares, and Semirara shares.

**[04:43:03.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003804)  
Totaling 60 billion among others.

**[04:43:07.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003805)  
So, yun po yung projected nila na privatization revenues for 2027.

**[04:43:13.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003806)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[04:43:16.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003807)  
Ultimately, the challenge is not simply to reduce the deficit or keep that below a particular numerical threshold.

**[04:43:25.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003808)  
It is to place our debt on a path toward stabilization and eventual decline without sacrificing

**[04:43:33.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003812)  
the economic growth that makes that objective possible. So the goal is not simply to spend less today, but to build a stronger economy that can generate more revenues.

**[04:43:47.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003813)  
In crafting that 2027 budget, we must make targeted investment in the foundations of economic growth, reliable and affordable energy, better infrastructure, human capital education, support for productive industries, MSMEs, faster and more predictable government processes,

**[04:44:14.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003814)  
and an environment that attracts both domestic and international demand.

**[04:44:17.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003815)  
And of course, this is on top of efforts improving the efficiency of public spending, so that every peso of government expenditure contributes as much as possible productivity and job creation.

**[04:44:34.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003816)  
That is the balance we should pursue as we move forward with this debate.

**[04:44:38.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003817)  
Those are all my questions, Mr. Speaker and Madam Sponsor. Thank you for the opportunity.

**[04:44:43.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003818)  
Thank you very much.

**[04:44:47.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003819)  
Mr. Speaker, thank you very much to our distinguished colleague, the Honorable Presley De Jesus.

**[04:44:51.100] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-003820)

**[04:55:01.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004013)

**[04:55:37.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004017)

**[04:55:39.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004018)

**[04:55:42.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004019)

**[04:55:43.920] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004020)  
Binanggit ko na po, ang natitira na lang natin ngayon ay Agus and Pulangi.

**[04:55:48.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004021)  
Ang tinutukoy ko po, Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, na i-review yung na-privatize na magkano yung natanggap natin acquisition cost,

**[04:56:00.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004022)  
magkano yung ginastos lang nila na na-rehabilitate din yung binta nila ngayon per kilowatt hour,

**[04:56:09.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004023)

**[04:56:09.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004024)

**[04:56:12.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004025)

**[04:56:18.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004026)

**[04:56:24.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004027)

**[04:56:31.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004028)

**[04:56:39.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004029)

**[04:56:50.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004030)

**[04:56:58.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004031)  
para po ma-evaluate din po ng ating economic managers.

**[04:57:01.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004032)

**[04:57:08.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004033)  
Kasi tinitimula.

**[04:57:09.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004034)  
Tingnan ko yung presyo, umiiyak yung mga taga-liti natin.

**[04:57:14.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004035)

**[04:57:20.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004036)

**[04:57:24.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004037)

**[04:57:26.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004038)

**[04:57:35.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004039)

**[04:57:45.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004041)

**[04:57:52.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004042)

**[04:57:57.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004043)

**[04:57:58.100] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004044)  
So, ang susunod ko pong katanungan, Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor,

**[04:58:09.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004045)  
ito po punta sa ating 2027 budget.

**[04:58:17.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004046)  
Balikan ko po sandali yung 2026.

**[04:58:23.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004047)  
Tinalakay na natin yung 2027, pero kaya po ba nating punduhan yung 2026 natin na budget?

**[04:58:32.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004048)  
Yung ga-an natin sa 2026, kaya po ba nating punduhan lahat yun?

**[04:58:39.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004049)  
So, ang susunod ko pong katanungan, Mr. Speaker,

**[04:58:44.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004050)  
What I'm asking distinguished sponsor, Mr. Speaker, is that

**[04:58:49.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004051)  
before we discuss the current budget intended for 2027,

**[04:58:57.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004052)

**[04:59:07.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004053)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[04:59:09.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004055)  
With us, our National Treasurer, sorry to quote our National Treasurer, but she gives the assurance that we will be able to fund our 2026 budget.

**[04:59:21.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004056)  
Okay, maganda naman pong ganoon pala, kaya naman pala natin.

**[04:59:29.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004057)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, nakalimutan ko pa po yung isang nakapublish na yung Diliman property ng Napokor.

**[04:59:41.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004058)  
Ang hindi ko rin maintindihan dyan, bakit ibinta natin, nandyan yung Napokor, nandyan nakaopisina yung Transco, ang ginawa, pinalipat si Napokor, nagririnta na lang ng 3.5 million a month.

**[04:59:59.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004059)  
Pero mayroon naman.

**[05:00:00.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004060)  
silang opisina. So siguro isama na rin pong tingnan yan ng mga economic

**[05:00:06.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004061)  
managers natin. Although hindi po siya ganun kabigat doon sa pag-privatize

**[05:00:13.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004062)  
ng isang planta. Kasi yung planta talaga nag-degenerate ka agad ng income

**[05:00:17.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004063)  
yun. Tapos nag-contribute pa na mapababa yung cost of electricity natin.

**[05:00:25.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004064)  
Pero isama rin 'yan sa privatization kasi naalala ko kasama yung

**[05:00:31.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004065)  
diliman property ng napokor. So for my next point, Mr. Speaker, since you

**[05:00:45.660] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004066)  
categorically answer na kaya naman po nating punduhan yung 2026

**[05:00:55.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004067)  
budget natin or yung ga-a, in 2027, ito po ay people-centered.

**[05:01:07.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004068)  
Sabi nga ni Secretary Kim ng DBM, ang meaning nito: Dapat ang Budget Makatao.

**[05:01:20.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004069)  
So tama naman po yun. I agree with him. So may we ask the

**[05:01:25.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004070)  
good sponsor to explain what objective or criteria, no, such as the

**[05:01:32.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004071)  
Philippine Development Plan for 2023 to 2028, sectoral plans, no, that needs

**[05:01:40.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004072)  
assessments, feasibility study, and measurable outcomes. We're used to

**[05:01:46.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004073)  
classify projects and programs as priorities across economic, social, defense,

**[05:01:53.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004074)  
general public service.

**[05:01:55.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004075)  
We also need to consider the debt-burdened sectors. Natanong na po ito kanina. Medyo pasensya na po, Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor, medyo nakulangan ako doon sa sagot kasi I agree with Congressman Tinio na medyo may mga nilagyan tayo ng budget na

**[05:02:23.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004076)  
Hindi naman totoong revenue generating yung contributor to economic growth na sana kung inuna natin yung ibang sektor na contributing to the economic growth, makikinabang din naman yung mga targeted subsidized sector.

**[05:02:49.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004077)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, amid very limited fiscal space, increasing borrowing costs and growing debt levels, kinailangan pong mag-prioritize ng DBM sapagkat P[unclear] trillion po yung kabuuang budget proposals na dumating po sa kanila. So they had to prioritize P7.2 trillion out of that. The criteria that they used po, they employed five criteria:

**[05:03:17.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004078)  
First is alignment with the Philippine Development Plan. Second is shovel readiness. Third is absorptive capacity. Fourth is fiscal space. And fifth po, prioritization of government programs that deliver the highest value and impact. So yun po yung limang kriteriya na ginamit po ng DBM.

**[05:03:39.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004079)  
Maraming salamat po, Distinguished Sponsor. Pupunta po ako doon sa absorptive capacity.

**[05:03:47.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004080)  
I totally agree na tama po yung kriteriya na yun. But I have a reservation, no? In reality, for example, may I cite the sitio electrification program of NEA.

**[05:04:03.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004081)  
Alam naman po natin yung economic contribution pag na-electrify natin yung isang lugar. Aside na nabigyan natin ng ginhawa yung mga tao.

**[05:04:17.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004082)  
Baka may mga negosyo. O hindi lang baka. In my experience, 43 years ako sa rural electrification, talagang umuunlad. Kaya nga yung mga barangay noon, nung nailawan na sa mga electric co-op yung area, nagiging munisipyo. Yung mga munisipyo nagiging city. Kaya malinaw po yung economic growth.

**[05:04:39.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004083)  
Pero pag-i-measure natin yung absorptive capacity ng NEA, dahil lang hindi nagastos yung...

**[05:04:47.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004084)  
Mayroon po tayong hindi nakita na dahilan. Nire-release po yung pundo, third quarter. Pag-release mo ng third quarter, sa bidding process pa lang, kakainin na niya yung buong third quarter.

**[05:05:10.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004085)  
Pag-release ng DBM sa third quarter yan, mag-umpisa na yung project.

**[05:05:17.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004086)  
Third quarter na, ay six months yung kailangan para ma-latag yung mga linya doon sa mga liblib na lugar. Ay talagang hindi niya ma-li-liquidate yun. Hindi ma-uubos.

**[05:05:30.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004087)  
Kaya kung titignan yan, ay hindi maganda yung absorptive capacity. Ang sinasabi ko po, ay tingnan yung mga gray area na yan. Kung i-release yan ng first quarter, I agree na kung 70 lang siya, 60 or less.

**[05:05:47.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004088)  
May problema siya sa absorptive capacity. Kasi we are talking here, doon sa example ko, of extending the lines to par-plunk and unviable areas na hindi po basta-basta.

**[05:05:59.660] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004089)  
Thank you.

**[05:06:17.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004090)  
May malaki po tayong inilagay dyan sa Barsi noong 2026 budget po kasi nag-release po tayo ng, if I'm not mistaken, 2 billion sa sityo electrification program po within NEA.

**[05:06:30.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004091)  
So, iti-check lang po ng pamilya natin sa DBM yung absorptive capacity utilization rate po ng NEA.

**[05:06:37.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004092)  
Ang tinutukoy ko lang, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, Distinguished Sponsor, ang ginawa ko lang example yung NEA.

**[05:06:47.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004093)  
But I know, there are a lot of government agencies na hindi on time na-release yung kanilang budget appropriation.

**[05:06:58.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004094)  
Kaya, yung kriteriya natin na measurement na absorptive capacity, mayroon akong reservation.

**[05:07:09.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004095)  
Kasi kung sasabi ko, third quarter muna i-release yan, tapos yung project nila, DICT halimbawa,

**[05:07:15.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004096)  
e, bibiding pa nila yung mga kawan, yung mga supplier, yung mga contractor, ay hindi talaga nila ma-liquidate yan at the end of the fiscal year.

**[05:07:31.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004097)  
So, magre-replic yan na mababa yung absorptive capacity nila.

**[05:07:36.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004098)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, upon conferring with DBM, yes, they recognize that.

**[05:07:42.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004099)  
Kasi po, halimbawa, second quarter na.

**[05:07:45.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004100)  
Or, paminsan third quarter na po nare-release yung SARO, talaga po natatamaan po yung utilization rate ng ahensya sa buong taon.

**[05:07:56.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004101)  
So, they will look into it po and make sure that instead of doing, siguro, a straight line assessment,

**[05:08:05.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004102)  
medyo baka pwedeng pro-rated yung approach para hindi naman po masaktan talaga yung utilization rate ng ahensya

**[05:08:13.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004103)  
dahil late na-release yung SARO.

**[05:08:15.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004104)  
So, DBM will look into it po.

**[05:08:17.960] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004105)  
I appreciate that. Sa lahat ng sekretary sa seven years ko dito, si Sekretary Kim lang ang umamin na titignan nila.

**[05:08:26.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004106)  
Lahat may mga ibang dahilan. So, thank you, Sekretary Kim, na titignan ninyo.

**[05:08:32.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004107)  
Lahat ng agency, ginawa ko lang example yung, ano, yung, actually, Mr. Speaker, may apat pa ako na issue.

**[05:08:41.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004108)  
Pero, papahingayin ko muna yung...

**[05:08:45.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004109)  
favorite person ko. So, hindi ko na itatanong tong iba. Hindi ko na itatanong.

**[05:08:52.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-004110)  
So, thank you, Mr. Speaker. Thank you sa mga sagot, Sekretary Kim, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:08:58.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004111)  
Thank you very much po to our distinguished colleague, Kong Sergio Dagooc. Thank you very much po.

**[05:09:04.500] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-004112)  
Thank you, the Honorable Dagooc. Majority Leader.

**[05:09:09.180] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-004113)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable...

**[05:09:12.100] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-004114)  
Honorable Elijah San Fernando from Partylist Kamanggagawa to interplay it on the proposed budget on the debate on general principles and provisions.

**[05:09:25.760] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-004115)  
The Honorable Elijah San Fernando of Partylist Kamanggagawa is recognized. Please proceed.

**[05:09:32.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004116)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Speaker, at sa ating kagalang-galang na sponsor, Chairperson Mika.

**[05:09:39.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004117)  
Ang una kong katanungan...

**[05:09:42.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004118)  
Madam Sponsor, Mr. Speaker, kinikilala po ng Estado ang fundamental na karapatan ng mga Pilipino sa impormasyon po yung People's Right to Information,

**[05:09:55.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004119)  
Article 3, Section 7 of 1987 Constitution.

**[05:09:59.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004120)  
And this simply means, Madam Sponsor, that transparency is paramount in all government proceedings, including the budget deliberations.

**[05:10:09.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004121)  
Now, Madam Sponsor, the...

**[05:10:12.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004122)  
The general provisions contain six separate transparency sections.

**[05:10:17.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004123)  
Can the sponsor point to a single provision in the HGAB or the 2027 NEP that gives an ordinary citizen or a duly accredited organization the right to be heard on record before an appropriation is finalized?

**[05:10:39.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004124)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[05:10:42.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004125)  
Madam Sponsor, Madam Sponsor.

**[05:10:56.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004126)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, there are two stages where there is involvement, not in the GAAP, but the first stage would be during the budget call.

**[05:11:08.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004127)  
So during the budget preparation stage...

**[05:11:12.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004128)  
yun noong budget calendar ng DBM na meron pong consultation sa atin pong mga civil society organizations

**[05:11:21.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004129)  
or kung sino po yung gusto mag-participate in the budget process.

**[05:11:24.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004130)  
In the budget process, on that part po, there is section 54 which pertains to Philippine Open Government Partnership.

**[05:11:40.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004131)  
So, departments, bureaus, offices, and instrumentalities of the national government, etc.,

**[05:11:46.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004132)  
shall adopt the open government partnership values of transparency, accountability, and citizen participation.

**[05:11:53.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004133)  
So, doon po, nabanggit po yun, section 54.

**[05:11:57.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004134)  
We also have it not during the budget deliberations in Congress because that is a legislative function.

**[05:12:05.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004135)  
But while that's not in the GA itself or in the special...

**[05:12:10.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004136)  
or general provisions, we've adopted that, we've institutionalized that.

**[05:12:16.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004137)  
Within the House po, we've formed the task force for people's participation, we've done the people's budget review,

**[05:12:22.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004138)  
we are refining the engagement models with civil society organizations.

**[05:12:28.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004139)  
So, meron din pong ganong engagement.

**[05:12:30.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004140)  
And then, when it comes to implementation, after the GA is passed, marami pong special provisions na nakakalat po sa iba't-ibang ahensya

**[05:12:38.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004141)  
na nag...

**[05:12:40.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004142)  
that allows people's audit for monitoring purposes.

**[05:12:44.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004143)  
I'm sure meron po under the special provision of the Department of Agriculture, for example,

**[05:12:51.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004144)  
wherein a people's audit is allowed for infrastructure and for up-to-market road projects.

**[05:12:57.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004145)

**[05:13:01.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004146)  
Na merong konsultasyon na nagaganap sa agency level bago po umakyat o pumasok dito sa Kongreso.

**[05:13:09.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004147)

**[05:13:10.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004148)  
the organizations would agree with me, and in fact, partner po natin, ang karamihan sa kanila,

**[05:13:16.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004149)  
na kadalasan po, dito sa mga budget call ng mga ahensya ng gobyerno,

**[05:13:21.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004150)  
hindi naman po talaga napaka-meaningful ng participation ng mga civil society organizations.

**[05:13:26.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004151)  
More often than not, nagiging stampad lang yung mga civil society organizations para lang masabi na merong participation.

**[05:13:33.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004152)

**[05:13:40.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004153)  
Maganda po, Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, na binanggit ninyo na merong mga mekanismo tungkol sa transparency after the funds are already spent.

**[05:13:51.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004154)  
In fact, if we are going to read together all the sections pertaining to transparency, lahat ito ay pumapatungkol kapag na-spent na o na-gasos na yung pondo.

**[05:14:04.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004155)  
So walang mga probisyon na pumapatungkol doon sa participation ng mga organizations.

**[05:14:10.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004156)  
Inari yung Pilipino o na mga people's organizations, civil society organizations, doon sa proseso ng pagbalangkas pa lang ng budget.

**[05:14:18.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004157)

**[05:14:23.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004158)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, yes, we recognize that point.

**[05:14:27.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004159)

**[05:14:36.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004160)  
That's why there are no general or special provisions.

**[05:14:39.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004161)  
That pertain in the deliberation and the formulation of the budget itself.

**[05:14:46.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004162)  
So, yun po, kung titinan po natin, lahat po ng mga general provisions at special provisions na nakapaloob sa budget ay patungkol po sa budget implementation.

**[05:30:00.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004945)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, inimbita po ang mga civil society organizations to present yung tinatawag na Alternative Budget Initiative, ABI.

**[05:30:11.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004946)  
At ang mga civil society organizations na po na ito noon ay kasama at kaibigan pa rin po natin hanggang ngayon.

**[05:30:18.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004947)  
These are the Social Watch Philippines and other civil society organizations.

**[05:30:21.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004948)  
So ito po yung pinupunto natin Madam Sponsor, Mr. Speaker, na pagdating po sa pagre-reforma patungkol sa transparency, accountability, at citizens' participation,

**[05:30:32.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004949)  
our CSOs demand not a tokenistic, hindi po tokenistic na ibibigay sa kanila na isang espasyo kung saan magiging observer lang po sila,

**[05:30:42.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004950)  
o pagpapasahin natin ang mga papel patungkol dun sa mga reformang gusto natin.

**[05:30:47.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004951)  
So ano po ang tingin dito ng kagalang-galang na sponsor, Mr. Speaker?

**[05:30:52.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004952)  
Madam Speaker?

**[05:30:54.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004953)  
Mr. Speaker, the engagement guidelines po, to be honest, are not drafted by the Committee on Appropriations.

**[05:31:01.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004954)  
In terms of what would be the level of participation po, again, that is under the purview of the CPBRD.

**[05:31:07.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004955)  
But I have the greatest respect for the Honorable Edson Lagman, and I really admire all the reforms that he put forth when he was Chairman of the Committee on Appropriations.

**[05:31:19.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004956)  
So we will look into it po.

**[05:31:22.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004957)  
Again, we started it last year to engage the civil society organizations after so many, like more than a decade of not having engaged them.

**[05:31:33.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004958)  
So please give us some time po.

**[05:31:35.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004959)  
We will look into all of these engagement models.

**[05:31:40.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004960)  
Here with us is Representative Chrisel.

**[05:31:43.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004961)  
You know po how much I love Kong Edsel.

**[05:31:45.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004962)  
So we will look at all of these and the models that were employed during the time of Chairperson.

**[05:31:51.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004963)  
Edsel Lagman.

**[05:31:52.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004964)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[05:31:56.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004965)  
Ang nais lamang po nating ipunto dito, hindi naman po natin po pwedeng laging ituro na CPBRD ang nag-head o nagsispearhead ng maganyang reformang o aktibidad.

**[05:32:07.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004966)  
Dapat po ang mismong Committee on Appropriations ang siyang nangunguna at nagtitiyak na ang ating mga kasama sa civil society organizations

**[05:32:15.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004967)  
ay nagiging kabahagi o aktibong kabahagi ng pagbabalangkas ng ating pambansang budget.

**[05:32:21.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004968)  
Now Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker,

**[05:32:23.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004969)  
will the Sponsor accept an amendment at the appropriate time requiring that the detailed breakdown of every lump sum appropriation including the LGSF and the FALGU be published in machine readable form under the Unified Accounts Code Structure at the time the National Expenditure Program is transmitted to Congress?

**[05:32:51.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004970)

**[05:32:55.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004971)

**[05:33:05.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004972)  
Thank you, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[05:33:07.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004973)

**[05:33:16.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004974)

**[05:33:21.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004975)

**[05:33:28.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004976)  
Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[05:33:31.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004977)

**[05:33:40.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004978)  
So we are checking po with DBM as they are the source for that particular table.

**[05:33:45.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004979)

**[05:34:02.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004983)

**[05:34:14.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004985)

**[05:34:19.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004986)

**[05:34:21.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004987)

**[05:34:24.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004988)

**[05:34:32.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004990)

**[05:34:36.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004991)

**[05:34:58.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004998)  
Section 106 allows the Commission on Audit until 31st October of the following year

**[05:35:04.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004999)

**[05:35:12.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005000)

**[05:35:17.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005001)

**[05:35:21.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005002)

and the question is,

**[05:36:50.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005023)  
questions on 4.55 billion in confidential and intelligence funds when unasked.

**[05:37:03.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005025)

**[05:37:26.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005029)

**[05:37:38.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005030)

**[05:37:46.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005031)

**[05:38:23.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005039)

**[05:40:11.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005059)

**[05:41:19.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005067)

Madam Speaker.

**[05:57:43.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005237)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, this is now about confidential funds.

**[05:57:47.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005238)  
Article 2, Section 28 of the 1987 Constitution says that subject to reasonable conditions prescribed by law,

**[05:57:55.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005239)  
the state adopts and implements a policy of full public disclosure of all its transactions involving public interest.

**[05:58:03.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005240)  
While Article 3, Section 7 states the right of the people to information on matters of public concern shall be recognized.

**[05:58:11.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005241)  
However, the General Assembly...

**[05:58:13.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005242)  
The General Principles and Provisions of House Bill 10585 again authorize lump sum confidential and intelligence appropriations across various agencies of the national government.

**[05:58:26.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005243)  
And under Joint Circular No. 2015-01 between DBMs, COA, and other agencies, disbursements from these funds are supported by documentary evidence kept in sealed envelopes.

**[05:58:39.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005244)  
Liquidation is subjected only to post-audit by the COA.

**[05:58:43.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005246)  
The COA Intelligence and Confidential Fund Audit Unit and a notice of this allowance may be issued years after the fact.

**[05:58:50.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005247)  
A delay now glaringly exposed as the impeachment proceedings reveal questionable confidential fund disbursements that escape timely scrutiny.

**[05:59:02.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005248)  
Can the sponsor therefore explain the wisdom of retaining confidential fund provisions in the 2027 GAB and its general principles

**[05:59:12.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005249)  
when the existing...

**[05:59:13.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005250)  
When the existing audit architecture inherently guarantees that illegal or unconstitutional spending can remain concealed for years, defeating the constitutional demand for public accountability, Madam Speaker?

**[05:59:43.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005251)  
When the existing audit architecture inherently guarantees that illegal or unconstitutional spending can remain concealed for years, defeating the constitutional demand for public accountability, Madam Speaker?

**[05:59:57.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005252)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, the...

**[06:00:26.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005254)

**[06:00:45.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005255)

**[06:00:53.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005256)

**[06:00:56.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005257)

**[06:01:12.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005258)

**[06:01:23.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005259)

**[06:01:35.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005261)

**[06:01:42.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005262)

**[06:01:49.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005263)

**[06:01:56.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005264)

**[06:02:07.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005265)

**[06:02:13.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005266)

**[06:02:26.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005267)

**[06:02:45.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005268)

**[06:02:54.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005269)  
So, yun lang po. Linimitahan naman na po.

**[06:02:56.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005270)  
So, yung bilang ng mga ahensya, sinigurado po ng DBM sa pagbuo po ng 2027 NEP,

**[06:03:05.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005271)

**[06:03:11.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005272)

**[06:03:17.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005273)

**[06:03:22.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005274)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[06:03:24.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005275)  
Madam Speaker, perhaps we still need to sharply interrogate whose security is actually being served

**[06:03:36.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005277)  
but we will reserve those questions to the agencies that are in question.

**[06:03:41.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005278)

**[06:03:46.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005279)  
yes, there were, there's proof, and nakita natin kung paano naabuso yung confidential expenses.

**[06:03:54.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005280)

**[06:03:59.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005281)

**[06:04:03.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005282)

**[06:04:08.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005283)

**[06:04:16.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005284)

**[06:04:23.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005285)

**[06:04:25.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005293)

**[06:04:34.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005294)  
which can happen, you know, years later.

**[06:04:36.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005295)  
Are there no mechanisms in place that DBCC or the DBM can exercise

**[06:04:42.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005296)  
other than what I've specified, Madam Speaker, good sponsor?

**[06:04:45.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005297)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, yes, the DBCC family recognizes that point,

**[06:04:54.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005298)  
that at this point, there are, nandun lang po, nasa level lang po nang kuha.

**[06:05:01.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005299)

**[06:05:08.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005300)  
So, yun po, we recognize that point.

**[06:05:10.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005301)  
So, Madam Speaker, good sponsor, there are no preventive mechanisms in place

**[06:05:15.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005302)  
in the existing rules, like in the 2015 Joint Circular,

**[06:05:19.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005303)

**[06:05:21.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005304)

**[06:05:28.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005305)

**[06:05:34.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005306)  
is submitted to DBM, Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[06:06:15.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005308)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, upon conferring with DBM,

**[06:06:20.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005309)  
so, the only thing that is submitted to them when the releases are requested

**[06:06:25.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005310)

**[06:06:28.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005311)

**[06:06:33.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005312)

**[06:06:39.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005313)

**[06:06:43.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005314)

**[06:06:45.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005315)

**[06:06:48.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005316)

**[06:06:52.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005317)

**[06:06:58.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005318)

**[06:07:03.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005319)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor,

**[06:07:06.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005320)  
so, essentially, we hope that COA does a good job.

**[06:07:09.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005321)  
But at the end of the day, this means that there can be years

**[06:07:13.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005322)  
wherein these disbursements,

**[06:07:15.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005323)  
these disbursements happen at kung naspotan ng COA

**[06:07:21.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005324)  
yung anomalous spending,

**[06:07:24.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005325)  
dito lang po magsisimulang investigasyon

**[06:07:27.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005326)  
at dito po magkakaroon ng chance na matignan

**[06:07:31.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005327)  
at syempre yung pinakagusto nating mangyari, mabawi.

**[06:07:35.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005328)  
So, as it stands, Madam Speaker, good sponsor,

**[06:07:38.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005329)  
not only does this representation see that confidential expenses, confidential funds,

**[06:07:45.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005330)  
is not in line with our constitutional principles

**[06:07:51.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005331)  
of accountability, of transparency,

**[06:07:55.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005332)  
of full public disclosure,

**[06:07:57.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005333)  
of all the state's transactions involving public interests.

**[06:08:02.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005334)  
We also believe that confidential funds and expenses

**[06:08:05.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005335)

it was a time of fiscal consolidation

**[06:35:36.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005589)  
during the term of

**[06:35:38.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005590)

**[06:35:40.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005591)  
wherein which budget

**[06:35:42.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005592)

**[06:35:44.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005593)

**[06:35:46.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005594)

**[06:35:48.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005595)  
During the

**[06:35:50.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005596)

**[06:35:52.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005597)  
period, during the

**[06:35:54.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005598)

**[06:35:56.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005599)

**[06:35:58.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005600)  
it was at an average of

**[06:36:00.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005601)  
3.5%.

**[06:36:02.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005602)  
But after nung

**[06:36:04.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005603)  
2019, nagkaroon tayo

**[06:36:06.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005604)  
ng pandemic,

**[06:36:08.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005605)  
tapos nun,

**[06:36:10.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005606)  
that's 2000, well,

**[06:36:12.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005607)  
2021, 22 naman

**[06:36:14.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005608)  
was the rebound, kaya

**[06:36:16.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005609)  
tumaas ng unti. Pero

**[06:36:18.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005610)  
ngayon,

**[06:36:20.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005611)  
how

**[06:36:22.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005612)  
do we intend to

**[06:36:24.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005613)  
be able to

**[06:36:26.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005614)  
support such

**[06:36:28.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005615)  
budget?

**[06:37:02.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005617)  
Mr. Speaker, my

**[06:37:04.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005618)  
distinguished colleague,

**[06:37:06.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005619)  
we recognize the point of

**[06:37:08.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005620)  
our distinguished colleague,

**[06:37:10.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005621)  
but the goal is to

**[06:37:12.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005622)  
do fiscal consolidation

**[06:37:14.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005623)  
down the line, and the

**[06:37:16.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005624)  
goal is to reduce

**[06:37:18.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005625)  
deficit to

**[06:37:20.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005626)  
From 5.4 in 2026 to 2030, sorry, just 3.5 in 2030.

**[06:37:31.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005627)  
How do we intend to do it?

**[06:37:34.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005628)  
Dahil if we look at the current economic situation,

**[06:37:43.280] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005629)  
we have GDP is down to around 2.4%, inflation is up.

**[06:37:55.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005630)  
So, paano pa natin maibababa yung, how do we plan to accomplish it?

**[06:38:03.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005631)  
Kasi ang sakin lang, instead of belt tightening, given the current situation, ito yung concern.

**[06:38:07.940] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005632)  
In 2024, the national debt was at 16.5 trillion.

**[06:38:12.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005633)  
For 2025, it increased to 17.71 trillion.

**[06:38:16.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005634)  
For 2026, it's now up to 19.39 trillion.

**[06:38:22.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005635)  
Now, and it will not stop here.

**[06:38:25.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005636)  
Dahil from the government's own BESF projects, budget expenditures and source of finance,

**[06:38:39.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005637)  
it presupposes that,

**[06:38:41.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005638)  
it will go up, the debt will go up to 19.77 trillion at the end of 2026,

**[06:38:49.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005639)  
and further up to 21.48 trillion at the end of 2027.

**[06:38:56.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005640)  
Now, dollar now is at, kanina nag-close, I think at 62.80.

**[06:39:01.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005641)  
We should also factor it in dahil it will have a big effect on debt servicing.

**[06:39:25.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005648)  
Madam Speaker, my distinguished colleague, upon conferring with economic managers,

**[06:39:31.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005649)  
really it's fiscal consolidation and really targeting quality spending,

**[06:39:39.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005650)  
looking at allocating funds.

**[06:39:41.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005652)  
towards items, programs, projects that have high economic multipliers.

**[06:39:46.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005653)  
So the goal is for us to be able to leverage on that to accelerate growth.

**[06:39:52.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005654)  
On the other hand po, with regard to the macroeconomic conditions

**[06:39:55.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005655)  
that were cited by our distinguished colleagues,

**[06:39:57.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005656)  
the projection is that by 2028,

**[06:40:02.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005657)  
these magre-reverse somehow in a more positive way

**[06:40:08.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005658)  
in terms of the macroeconomic factors surrounding it po.

**[06:40:13.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005659)  
So hopefully, inflation would lessen,

**[06:40:16.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005660)  
the foreign exchange rate would be more favorable,

**[06:40:19.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005661)  
the price of fuel will go down.

**[06:40:22.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005662)  
So with that po, coupled with quality spending,

**[06:40:25.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005663)  
ma-be-bridge po yung gap doon po sa ating deficit.

**[06:40:28.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005664)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[06:40:29.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005665)  
I hope that our economic managers have the same positive view.

**[06:40:38.180] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005666)  
Kasi,

**[06:40:38.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005667)  
if we want to be realistic,

**[06:40:41.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005668)  
I'm sure that they all know it is unwise

**[06:40:43.940] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005669)  
to go on a big budget deficit spending

**[06:40:48.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005670)  
if there is already inflationary pressure.

**[06:40:51.620] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005671)  
And pagka hindi nararamdaman ng economic managers natin yung inflationary pressure,

**[06:40:56.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005672)  
then there's something wrong with our government.

**[06:41:00.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005673)  
Walang mangyayari sa atin if we do not feel

**[06:41:03.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005674)  
that there is economic pressure already dahil

**[06:41:06.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005675)  
ano yung ekonomiya,

**[06:41:08.180] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005676)  
economic pressure natin.

**[06:41:10.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005677)  
Sa FDI na lang,

**[06:41:12.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005678)  
makano yung foreign direct investment natin ngayon?

**[06:41:15.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005679)  
From a high of, I think, 9.7 billion way back in 2024,

**[06:41:19.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005680)  
it's down to 3.4 billion for 2026.

**[06:41:25.020] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005681)  
It's a good indicator of investor's confidence in our country.

**[06:41:29.580] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005682)  
Now, if we are not concerned about that,

**[06:41:32.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005683)  
then pupunta tayo, dapat tignan natin yung

**[06:41:34.980] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005684)  
what is the current,

**[06:41:38.180] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005685)  
projected inflation natin ngayon.

**[06:41:43.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005686)  
Diba?

**[06:41:45.000] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005687)  
It's also down.

**[06:41:46.620] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005688)  
We projected what?

**[06:41:49.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005689)  
It to be at a lower percentage,

**[06:41:53.480] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005690)  
but now it's,

**[06:41:54.760] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005691)  
nasa 6.1 ba?

**[06:41:58.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005692)  
Expected po, inflation is at around 5.4% in 2027.

**[06:42:06.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005693)  
Yan po yung,

**[06:42:06.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005694)  
naka-embed po,

**[06:42:09.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005695)  
na assumption sa budget.

**[06:42:13.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005696)  
And 3.3 and down to 3.3% in 2028.

**[06:42:19.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005697)  
Kasi,

**[06:42:20.180] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005698)  
kaya ako lang ito,

**[06:42:22.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005699)  
cause for concern,

**[06:42:24.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005700)  
if we really look at it,

**[06:42:27.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005701)  
for the second quarter 2026,

**[06:42:30.320] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005702)  
real GDP growth was 2.3%.

**[06:42:32.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005703)  
August inflation was 6.1%.

**[06:42:36.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005704)  
Bottom 30% of household inflation was actually 8.2%.

**[06:42:41.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005705)  
These are concerning data.

**[06:42:44.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005706)  
Kung titignan talaga natin.

**[06:42:47.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005707)  
Now, as I mentioned,

**[06:42:50.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005708)  
we also feel it.

**[06:42:51.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005709)  
Fuel prices alone is projected to go up to,

**[06:42:56.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005710)  
diesel prices projected to go up to as high as 100 pesos per liter.

**[06:43:02.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005711)  
That will have an economic impact.

**[06:43:03.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005712)  
In fact, makikita nyo,

**[06:43:05.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005713)  
mas,

**[06:43:06.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005714)  
less traffic ngayon,

**[06:43:07.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005715)  
especially yung mga public utility vehicles.

**[06:43:09.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005716)  
They,

**[06:43:11.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005717)  
not as much fly the roads anymore.

**[06:43:13.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005718)  
Dahil,

**[06:43:14.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005719)  
it's better to stay at home

**[06:43:15.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005720)  
dan maglabas ka,

**[06:43:17.320] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005721)  
bumiyahi ka dahil hindi ka kikita.

**[06:43:23.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005722)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[06:43:24.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005723)  
my distinguished colleague,

**[06:43:25.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005724)  
on the part of the economic managers,

**[06:43:27.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005725)  
they assure,

**[06:43:29.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005726)  
they give the assurance that they review

**[06:43:31.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005727)  
these macroeconomic assumptions every three months po.

**[06:43:34.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005728)  
So, quarterly naman,

**[06:43:36.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005729)  
depending on the realities that are prevailing,

**[06:43:40.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005730)  
in-update naman po nila yung macroeconomic assumptions.

**[06:43:43.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005731)  
Dahil,

**[06:43:44.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005732)  
well, yung sakin lang,

**[06:43:45.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005733)  
we have to look at the indicators.

**[06:43:48.560] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005734)  
Is our economy already in its full capacity?

**[06:43:52.560] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005735)  
Given the current rate,

**[06:43:53.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005736)  
I believe,

**[06:43:55.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005737)  
mahirap nang tumaas pa yan.

**[06:43:58.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005738)  
And it will go down,

**[06:43:59.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005739)  
given the current state.

**[06:44:01.280] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005740)  
We're also looking at 2028.

**[06:44:03.940] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005741)  
Anong ibig ko sabihin sa 2028?

**[06:44:06.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005742)  
It's an election year.

**[06:44:09.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005743)  
Investors,

**[06:44:10.180] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005744)  
both foreign and local,

**[06:44:12.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005745)  
usually,

**[06:44:13.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005746)  
they hold on investments.

**[06:44:15.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005747)  
Dahil, because of political uncertainty.

**[06:44:18.220] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005748)  
That is also something that we

**[06:44:19.620] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005749)  
have to consider.

**[06:44:21.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005750)  
Madali magtaas nang magtaas ng budget

**[06:44:23.980] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005751)  
pagka hindi natin pera yung ginagamit.

**[06:44:26.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005752)  
Like in a company,

**[06:44:28.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005753)  
when you run a company,

**[06:44:29.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005754)  
you don't just like approve a huge budget

**[06:44:32.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005755)  
if you know that it cannot be sustained.

**[06:44:34.940] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005756)  
Kasi ang nararamdaman,

**[06:44:36.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005757)  
we have to look at the state of our economy.

**[06:44:40.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005758)  
It's already, as I mentioned,

**[06:44:41.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005759)  
full capacity.

**[06:44:43.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005760)  
Now,

**[06:44:45.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005761)  
ang problema rin,

**[06:44:47.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005762)  
as we all know,

**[06:44:48.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005763)  
excess spending drives up inflation.

**[06:44:52.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005764)  
So,

**[06:44:53.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005765)  
kagaya ng sinabi ni Sheck Balizakan,

**[06:44:56.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005766)  
alam mo naman,

**[06:44:57.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005767)  
ang economy natin is consumption driven.

**[06:45:00.000] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005768)  
It's not money coming from external sources.

**[06:45:03.980] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005769)  
It's everyone's money just going around.

**[06:45:07.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005770)  
So, well, these are my concerns.

**[06:45:11.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005771)  
Dahil, which, because I would want to see more of the money that we approve

**[06:45:18.480] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005772)  
be used in programs and projects which would pump prime the economy.

**[06:45:25.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005773)  
Kasi dito sa 7.2 trillion, how much is actually available to pump prime the economy?

**[06:45:33.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005774)  
And what specific projects ang pupuntahan nito?

**[06:45:37.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005775)  
Because kagaya yung mga projects which are supposed to pump prime the economy,

**[06:45:42.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005776)  
North to South Railway, dapat natapos na pero hindi.

**[06:45:47.000] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005777)  
Subway natin, tinanggalan natin yan ng, nilagay natin sa amprogram what was already there.

**[06:45:52.940] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005778)  
And, ako, saan nilagay?

**[06:45:55.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005779)  
Nilagay sa ayuda.

**[06:45:57.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005780)  
But, it does not pump prime the economy.

**[06:46:01.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005781)

**[06:53:19.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005948)

**[06:53:30.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005949)

**[06:53:43.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005951)

**[06:53:54.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005952)  
which we can use as an indicator of the sustainability of our debt levels,

**[06:53:58.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005953)  
the goal po is for it to reduce from 58.6% to 56.1%, which is below the 70% benchmark po for the general government debt to GDP ratio.

**[06:54:13.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005954)  
So the goal po is little by little through fiscal consolidation and quality spending,

**[06:54:20.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-005955)  
which would hopefully spur growth, we'll be able to bridge the deficit po and make sure the debt is sustainable.

**[06:54:27.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005956)

**[06:54:40.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005957)  
If ang binabangga natin,

**[06:54:43.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005958)

**[06:54:49.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005959)

**[06:54:55.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005960)  
sa projected revenues na 5.2%, dahil hindi naman nakakadagdag sa ekonomiya natin yung mga ibang kinargahan nung ano eh,

**[06:55:06.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005961)

**[06:55:56.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005967)
