**[06:57:00.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005974)  
And hindi ko talaga mahanap yung punto na yun eh.

**[06:57:10.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005975)  
We have to understand that the 2027 budget assumes...

**[06:57:13.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005976)  
A fairly significant economic rebound.

**[06:57:16.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005977)  
Kaya nga, 7.2 trillion yung hinihingi, ang prinupose.

**[06:57:23.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005978)  
And kaya nga, sinasabi ngayon na kaya natin gawin yun.

**[06:57:27.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005979)  
Now assuming if the economy grows only 2 to 3%, rather than the 5 to 6%, which is projected, ano mangyayari sa atin?

**[06:57:39.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005980)  
Kunyari mag-maintain tayo for next year, 2027.

**[06:57:43.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005981)  
2 to 3% lang ang growth rate natin.

**[06:57:47.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005982)  
Not the 5 to 6% which is projected to support the 7.2 trillion budget.

**[06:57:53.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005983)  
Three things will happen.

**[06:57:56.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005984)  
Kasi three things becomes increasingly difficult.

**[06:57:59.580] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005985)  
Simultaneously hitting the 5.2 trillion in revenues, reducing the deficit to GDP ratio, and preventing the debt ratio from increasing further.

**[06:58:10.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005986)  
Ang magiging choice lang ng gobyerno,

**[06:58:13.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005987)  
Madam Chair, good sponsor,

**[06:58:15.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005988)  
paka nangyari yan, paka 2 to 3% lang tayo,

**[06:58:18.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005989)  
ang magiging choice lang ng gobyerno is to borrow more money for 2028,

**[06:58:24.660] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005990)  
raise additional revenues from taxes,

**[06:58:30.200] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005991)  
reduce expenditures,

**[06:58:31.760] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005992)  
or i-delay natin o i-hold yung mga appropriated spending.

**[06:58:38.540] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005993)  
That's the only way that you would be able to control it.

**[06:58:42.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005994)  
But...

**[06:58:43.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005995)  
Would that be feasible?

**[06:58:46.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005996)  
Well, Madam Chair, good sponsor,

**[06:58:48.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005997)  
I just want to remind everyone that

**[06:58:51.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005998)  
on the part of debt, foreign debt, or our national debt,

**[06:59:00.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005999)  
debt sustainability increasingly depends on government converting borrowed money into productive

**[06:59:10.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006000)  
income.

**[06:59:13.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006001)  
So, that's the only way that you would be able to control it.

**[06:59:16.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006003)  
But if we do not do it,

**[06:59:18.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006004)  
nga gastusin lang natin for recurring expenditures, kagaya pagkatinignan mo,

**[06:59:23.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006005)  
dito sa 7.2 trillion,

**[06:59:25.280] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006006)  
tignan natin yung breakdown.

**[06:59:26.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006007)  
1.982 trillion is for personal services,

**[06:59:31.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006008)  
2.944 trillion is for MOUE,

**[06:59:36.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006009)  
1.114 trillion is for interest and financial expenses.

**[06:59:41.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006010)  
So,

**[06:59:42.000] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006011)  
as correctly pointed out by Chair Mica,

**[06:59:45.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006012)  
what is left is 1.160 trillion for capital expenditure.

**[06:59:51.540] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006013)  
And if that 1.160 trillion is not allocated properly for projects that will prime our economy...

**[07:00:00.000] Deputy Speaker Maria Rachel J. Arenas** (asr-006014)  
Representative Suntay, can you wrap up?

**[07:00:03.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006015)  
Thank you. Madam Speaker, as I mentioned, pinagsama-sama na natin ito na 30 minutes each.

**[07:00:11.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006016)  
Yung sa DepDev, saka sa DBM, yung unang 30 minutes ko tapos na. Isang oras na lang ang matitira.

**[07:00:22.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006017)  
Madam Chair, good sponsor. Maraming maraming salamat to.

**[07:00:25.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006018)  
As I mentioned, I just needed to point this out because it is truly a cause for concern dahil kawawa naman yung susunod na henerasyon pagka palaki lang ng palaki yung utang at hindi naman natin kayang bayaran na o hindi kayang isustain ng ating ekonomiya.

**[07:00:47.200] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006019)  
Thank you very much.

**[07:00:47.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-006020)  
Thank you, Honorable Suntay.

**[07:00:48.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-006021)  
Quick response lang po, Madam Speaker. Very quick lang po.

**[07:00:51.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-006022)  
So, it's really going to be a combination of qualities.

**[07:00:55.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-006023)  
Depending on infrastructure projects and other items with economic multipliers and at the same time po, high economic multipliers and at the same time po, investments in human capital development.

**[07:01:06.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-006024)  
Hopefully po, the combination of both would help expand our production capacity and spur economic growth and hopefully that would help narrow down the deficit.

**[07:01:14.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-006025)  
But we recognize the points raised by the Honorable Bong Suntay. Maraming maraming salamat po.

**[07:01:20.000] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006026)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair.

**[07:01:23.460] Deputy Speaker Maria Rachel J. Arenas** (asr-006027)  
Majority Leader.

**[07:26:57.260] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006199)

**[07:26:57.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006200)  
The Department of Economy, Planning, and Development, or DepDev, and its attached agencies for fiscal year 2027, amounting to 18.474 billion pesos.

**[07:27:09.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006201)  
As a primary policy, planning, coordinating, and monitoring arm of the national government, DEPDev has continually assured the formulation of policies that pursue the objectives of the Philippine Development Plan 2023?2028

**[07:27:27.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006202)  
to shepherd economic

**[07:27:28.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006203)  
and social transformation through productive and strategic investments in the country?s social and economic sectors.

**[07:27:38.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006204)  
At a time when our country faces external headwinds that raise challenges to each segment of Philippine society,

**[07:27:42.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006205)  
it is incumbent upon our government and this Congress

**[07:27:46.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006206)  
to assure the continued operations of DEPDev in pursuit of its mandate

**[07:27:55.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006207)  
to facilitate policies that encourage local investment,

**[07:27:57.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006211)  
coordinate

**[07:27:59.260] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006212)  
the vertical and horizontal alignment

**[07:29:38.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006262)

**[07:29:42.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006264)

kumulang

**[07:30:00.000] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006273)  
5.06 billion para sa CBMS, para sa panibagong cycle.

**[07:30:09.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006275)

**[07:30:17.360] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006277)

**[07:30:19.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006278)

**[07:30:24.500] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006279)

**[07:30:28.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006280)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[07:30:58.200] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006281)

**[07:31:01.440] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006282)  
So nais ko po malaman, Madam Chair,

**[07:31:03.300] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006283)  
mula nung naisabatas ang CBMS,

**[07:31:05.920] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006284)  
magkano na po ang total appropriations na ininvest ng ating pamahalaan sa CBMS?

**[07:31:28.200] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006285)  
Madam Speaker, perhaps we could stop my time as we wait for the response from the sponsor.

**[07:31:38.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006286)  
Yes.

**[07:31:39.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006287)  
And then from 2024...

**[07:32:52.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006348)  
Madam Speaker, so from 2019 to 2023, given that we were only doing pilot programs for the CBMS, 500 million po yung inallocate natin for the CBMS.

**[07:33:14.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006349)  
And then for 2024, mayroon na pong 5 billion kasi full-fledged na po yung pag-implement because it was a census year. We conducted the census in 2024, so for 2025 and 2026, again 500 million annually.

**[07:33:31.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006350)  
And then for 2027, since we're also conducting the census for 2027, 5 billion din po ang i-allocate.

**[07:33:38.680] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006351)  
Maraming salamat po, distinguished sponsor. So, uulitin ko lang po, Madam Speaker.

**[07:33:43.740] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006352)  
Kung sakaling papayagan po natin na bigyan muli ng mahigit 5 bilyong piso ang CBMS,

**[07:33:50.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006353)  
ang ibig sabihin ito, mula nung naisabatas ang CBMS, magkakaroon po tayo ng more than 10 billion pesos that will be allocated to the CBMS.

**[07:34:00.680] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006354)  
Just one thing I want to make clear here, Madam Speaker. Hindi po ako tutol sa CBMS.

**[07:34:05.140] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006355)

**[07:34:07.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006356)  
When the CBMS Act was enacted into law, I thought that this would be a time wherein this would usher a new era

**[07:34:15.380] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006357)

**[07:34:21.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006358)  
Kaya nga po mula naging membro ako ng Kongreso, taon-taon, parating ko po tinatanong kung ano yung mga nagiging accomplishment ng CBMS

**[07:34:28.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006359)  
dahil nagsisilbi po itong gabay, hindi lamang sa LGU, kung hindi, sa bawat ahensya po ng pamahalaan.

**[07:34:36.420] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006360)  
Ngayon, ngayong nalaman po natin ang potential investment natin sa CBMS, gusto ko po malaman, Madam Speaker, kung ano ang nakuha natin kapalit nito.

**[07:34:47.140] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006361)  
Uulitin ko po, Madam Speaker, we've spent more than 5 billion pesos for the CBMS and we're looking to invest 5 billion pesos more.

**[07:34:55.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006362)  
It's very important for us to know kung ano ang kabalitan.

**[07:34:58.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006363)  
Kung ano ang buoang investment na makukuha natin kapalit nito.

**[07:35:04.140] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006364)  
Unang-una, Madam Speaker, importante ang CBMS dahil hindi nakuhulaan ng gobyerno kung saan ang kahirapan.

**[07:35:13.060] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006365)  
Hindi na kanyang-kanyang listahan ang bawat ahensya.

**[07:35:16.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006366)  
Hindi na dapat may hiwalay na listahan ang DSWD, ang ibang departamento, at ibang listahan ng LGU na hindi nagtutugma.

**[07:35:24.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006367)  
Kaya po ipinasa ang CBMS Act para magkaroon tayo ng ehem.

**[07:35:28.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006368)  
Updated, disaggregated, household level information na maaaring gamitin ng national government at local government

**[07:35:37.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006369)  
para malaman natin sino sa ating populasyon, sino sa pamayanan ang nangangailangan, nasaan sila, ano ang kailangan nila,

**[07:35:46.540] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006370)  
at saan dapat mapunta ang limitadong pondo ng gobyerno.

**[07:35:50.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006371)  
And after all these years, and after all these billions allocated, magandang malaman po natin, Madam Speaker,

**[07:35:58.220] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006372)  
kung na-deliverance.

**[07:35:58.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006373)  
Mag-deliver ba natin ang pangakong yun?

**[07:36:02.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006374)  
Madam Speaker, as I've said, taon-taon po natin sinusubaybayan ang CBMS.

**[07:36:07.560] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006375)  
As a matter of fact, during the deliberations for the 2025 budget, if my recollection is correct,

**[07:36:15.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006376)

**[07:36:26.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006377)

**[07:36:30.620] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006378)  
This is all on the record, Madam Speaker.

**[07:36:33.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006379)  
They said, October or December 2024, tatapusin na nila.

**[07:36:38.160] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006380)

**[07:36:40.400] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006381)

**[07:36:45.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006382)  
sinabi po sa atin na completed na ang 2024 CBMS covering approximately 26.3 million households.

**[07:36:54.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006383)  
Could the distinguished sponsor?

**[07:36:56.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006384)  
Could the distinguished sponsor confirm this?

**[07:37:00.700] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006385)  
This is all on the record, Madam Speaker.

**[07:37:03.260] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006386)  
Sinabi po sa atin ng PSA last year,

**[07:37:06.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006387)  
kumpleto na ang CBMS covering 26.3 million households.

**[07:37:13.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006388)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[07:37:15.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006389)  
Just to the first point,

**[07:37:17.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006390)  
we are one with our distinguished colleague that it's actually one of the mandates of the DepDep.

**[07:37:24.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006391)  
It's supposed to generate,

**[07:37:26.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006392)  
impartial and evidence-based analysis and programs that pursue national development.

**[07:37:32.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006393)  
So that is precisely the reason why we establish a CBMS.

**[07:37:37.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006394)  
Just with regards to the accomplishments of the CBMS,

**[07:37:41.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006395)  
this is from, according to the PSA,

**[07:37:44.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006396)  
26.9 million households were collected information from,

**[07:37:50.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006397)  
yeah, from out of the 29.7 million total households.

**[07:37:54.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006398)  
So that's 91%.

**[07:37:56.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006399)  
After that,

**[07:37:57.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006400)  
and 2.9 million households were then identified as poor.

**[07:38:03.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006401)  
Another 2.5 million households were identified as near poor.

**[07:38:07.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006402)  
So,

**[07:38:08.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006403)  
Madam Speaker,

**[07:38:09.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006404)  
could the distinguished sponsor confirm kung last year during the plenary budget deliberations,

**[07:38:16.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006405)  
the PSA mentioned here in the plenary that the CBMS was completed,

**[07:38:22.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006406)  
covering more than 26 million households.

**[07:38:24.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006407)  
This is all in the record.

**[07:38:25.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006408)  
And gusto ko lang ito.

**[07:38:25.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006409)  
And gusto ko lang po i-confirm ng distinguished sponsor na

**[07:38:28.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006410)  
yun po ang naging representation ng PSA dito sa Kongreso.

**[07:38:31.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006411)  
Kompleto na daw siya as of last year.

**[07:38:34.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006412)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[07:38:35.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006413)  
We confirm that we did indeed say that the CBMS was complete.

**[07:38:40.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006414)  
All data was transmitted to the DSWD for their evaluation on the conditionality.

**[07:38:48.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006415)  
Of course, for the assessments and so that they can be guided in terms of

**[07:38:53.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006416)  
what programs to evaluate.

**[07:38:54.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006417)  
What programs to institute.

**[07:38:56.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006418)  
With that being said,

**[07:38:58.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006419)  
the data that was collected because the CBMS was completed

**[07:39:03.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006420)  
was used for the following DSWD programs,

**[07:39:06.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006421)

**[07:39:08.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006422)  
the walang gutom program,

**[07:39:10.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006423)  
the social pension,

**[07:39:12.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006424)

**[07:39:16.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006425)

**[07:39:18.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006426)  
Madam Speaker,

**[07:39:19.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006427)  
and if I also remember correctly,

**[07:39:21.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006428)

**[07:39:23.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006429)

**[07:39:24.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006430)  
hindi lamang DSWD ang nag-request ng datos na ito.

**[07:39:27.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006431)  
At sinabi po ito ng budget sponsor last year.

**[07:39:30.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006432)  
Sinabi nila na noong,

**[07:39:32.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006433)  
for year 2026,

**[07:39:34.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006434)  
may mga ahensya po,

**[07:39:36.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006435)

**[07:39:37.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006436)

**[07:39:39.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006437)  
TESDA,

**[07:39:40.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006438)  
NCSC,

**[07:39:41.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006439)  
DepEd,

**[07:39:42.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006440)  
DOLE,

**[07:39:43.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006441)  
DOH,

**[07:39:44.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006442)  
kung saan naghihingi po sila ng datos.

**[07:39:47.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006443)  
So hindi lamang DSWD programs dito.

**[07:39:49.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006444)  
As a matter of fact,

**[07:39:50.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006445)  
the CBMS is the backbone

**[07:39:52.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006446)  
of all our programs.

**[07:39:54.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006447)  
Ito po yung datos na mag-guide,

**[07:39:56.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006448)  
hindi lamang sa ating mga policy makers,

**[07:39:58.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006449)  
kung hindi,

**[07:39:59.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006450)

**[07:40:01.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006451)  
And could the distinguished sponsor confirm this?

**[07:40:03.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006452)  
Hindi lamang DSWD,

**[07:48:23.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006677)  
at the,

**[07:48:24.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006678)  
on the spot, there has to be

**[07:48:27.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006679)  
a data privacy officer.

**[07:48:30.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006680)  
If not, then

**[07:48:31.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006681)  
the CBMS

**[07:48:34.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006682)  
data, which already

**[07:48:35.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006683)  
is completed, cannot be

**[07:48:37.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006684)  
fully turned over.

**[07:48:39.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006685)  
Madam Speaker, why didn't the PSA mention this

**[07:48:41.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006686)  
to us last year?

**[07:48:43.520] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006687)  
Kaya po ito napaka-importante sa akin, Madam Speaker,

**[07:48:46.040] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006688)  
dahil ngayong 20th Congress,

**[07:48:48.440] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006689)  
nagpasa po ako ng resolution,

**[07:48:50.520] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006690)  
nagbigay ako ng privilege speech,

**[07:48:52.480] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006691)  
dahil natuklasan ko na hindi pa pala

**[07:48:53.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006692)  
tapos yung CBMS. Kahit na sinabi nila

**[07:48:56.220] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006693)  
last year, natapos na siya.

**[07:48:57.540] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006694)  
So, yung sa akin lang po, Madam Speaker,

**[07:48:59.720] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006695)  
sana, sinabi ng PSA ito

**[07:49:01.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006696)  
sa atin, nung nakaraang taon,

**[07:49:04.240] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006697)  
para maiwasan natin yung mga ganitong problema.

**[07:49:05.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006698)  
So, yung tanong ko po, Madam Speaker,

**[07:49:07.780] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006699)  
ba't ngayon lang nila sa atin sinasabi

**[07:49:09.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006700)  
na may mga data privacy compliances

**[07:49:11.660] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006701)  
kaya hindi pa na ito turn over?

**[07:49:13.380] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006702)  
When? They could have said that during the budget

**[07:49:15.600] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006703)  
deliberations last year.

**[07:49:27.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006704)  
Yes, we understand, Madam Speaker,

**[07:49:37.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006705)  
the concern of our distinguished colleague.

**[07:49:40.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006706)  
Really, from the 2025 deliberations,

**[07:49:45.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006707)  
it was really because of this requirement

**[07:49:48.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006708)  
for the data privacy officers,

**[07:49:50.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006709)  
which we thought would not be a hindrance

**[07:49:53.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006710)  
to the full turnover of CBMS data.

**[07:49:58.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006711)  
And that's the reason why

**[07:50:00.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006712)  
at the time,

**[07:50:02.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006713)  
the requirement wasn't

**[07:50:04.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006714)  
fully implemented.

**[07:50:08.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006715)  
And then,

**[07:50:10.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006716)  
over the course of

**[07:50:11.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006717)  
the turnover to the LGUs,

**[07:50:13.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006718)  
it became a requirement.

**[07:50:14.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006719)  
Madam Speaker, ano po ang measured error rate

**[07:50:16.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006720)  
ng CBMS?

**[07:50:20.000] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006721)  
Ilan ang duplicate?

**[07:50:21.500] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006722)  
Ilan ang missing household?

**[07:50:23.200] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006723)  
Ilan ang maliang household composition?

**[07:50:25.320] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006724)  
Ilan maliang employment status?

**[07:50:26.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006725)  
ilan ang maling socioeconomic information, may mga tao ba sa database na sinasabing patay na pero buhay pa pala.

**[07:50:34.480] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006726)  
Gusto ko pong malaman dahil napaka-importante po nito habang pinaplano natin ang budget ng bawat departamento na umaasa sa datos.

**[07:50:42.740] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006727)  
At napaka-importante po, Madam Speaker ng CBMS, dahil isa po itong gabay para sigurado tayo na ang mga pulisiyan natin sinasalamin niya ang pangangailangan ng pamayanan.

**[07:50:56.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006728)  
Madam Speaker, so the CBMS is a census.

**[07:51:06.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006729)  
So after which the census is conducted, the PSA then checks with the death registry.

**[07:51:14.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006730)  
So that is the way in which we check at least for the deaths recorded.

**[07:51:23.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006731)  
Yes, but in terms of the margin of error,

**[07:51:26.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006732)  
it's really a continuous back-to-forth, I think it's a case-by-case basis regarding which constituency or which area, which jurisdiction would be subject to this.

**[07:51:40.440] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006733)  
So up to now, Madam Speaker, wala pa po tayong precise number kung ano yung margin of error dun sa mga datos na nakokolekta natin mula sa CBMS.

**[07:51:48.080] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006734)  
Wala po tayong error rate, wala po tayong metric or data sa mga kinokolekta natin kung mayroong erroneous na entry.

**[07:51:56.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006735)  
Wala po tayong idea.

**[07:52:00.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006736)  
Yes, just to clarify, Madam Speaker, the CBMS is a census, it's not a survey.

**[07:52:06.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006737)  
So it's literally a collection of data.

**[07:52:11.060] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006738)  
Madam Speaker, pwede ko po ba malaman, ano yung pinakamatandang household record na ginagamit pa rin ngayon bilang current CBMS information?

**[07:52:21.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006739)  
For example, gusto ko po malaman, Madam Speaker, ano yung average or median age?

**[07:52:26.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006740)  
Considering na hindi naman siya ginawa all at once. May mga census kayong ginawa sa ibang time frame.

**[07:52:34.200] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006741)  
So pwede po ba namin malaman, Madam Speaker, ano yung average or median age ng records?

**[07:52:41.580] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006742)  
May mga records po ba tayo galing mula 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025, 2026? Pwede po ba namin malaman yun, Madam Speaker?

**[07:52:56.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006743)  
2024 CBMS, Madam Speaker, is the oldest record that we have for using for PSA data.

**[07:53:19.920] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006744)  
Ano naman po, Madam Speaker, yung pinakabago? 2024 lang ba lahat?

**[07:53:26.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006745)  
Kasi naiintindihan ko po, Madam Speaker, the first cycle, POPs and CBMS 2024, I want to find out if there are certain household information collected beyond 2024.

**[07:53:44.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006746)  
Let's say last year, may mga sinensos ba kayo ng 2025? May mga sinensos ba kayo ng 2026?

**[07:53:50.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006747)  
Or 2024 lang ba lahat?

**[07:53:57.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006748)  
For social protection, Madam Speaker, the oldest would be 2024. But for the FIES, which is the Family Income and Expenditure Survey, it would be just for 2026.

**[07:54:14.440] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006749)  
So meron pong two-year interval yung FIES and for social protection.

**[07:54:20.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006750)  
For social services, is that correct?

**[07:54:22.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006751)  
Yes, two-year interval, Madam Speaker.

**[07:54:24.600] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006752)  
Okay. So, Madam Speaker, yung tanong ko po sa PSA, and of course, for the guidance of all our government agencies who are relying on the CBMS data,

**[07:54:34.820] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006753)  
malaking pagkakaiba po kasi yung mga nakokollect natin ng 2026 and 2024.

**[07:54:41.520] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006754)  
Dahil sa isang household, in the span of two years, a lot can change. May mga nawawala ng trabaho, may mga lumilipat.

**[07:54:50.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006755)  
So, yung tanong po dito, masyado na bang luma ang household information, let's say, ng 2024 para gamitin sa beneficiary targeting?

**[07:55:01.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006756)  
And I also want to know, Madam Speaker, maglalaan tayo muli ng 5 billion para sa CBMS census, kailan po yung target date natin para isagawa ang second cycle?

**[07:55:20.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006757)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[07:55:42.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006758)  
Madam Speaker, the agency agrees that given the long interval,

**[07:55:50.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006759)  
we can't fully capture the shocks that we experience over the course of those two years.

**[07:55:58.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006760)  
So, that's precisely the reason why for 2027, we're asking for 5 billion, because of the shocks that happened in 2025 and also, of course, 2026.

**[07:56:09.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006761)  
So, really, the 5 billion for the CBMS for 2027, besides the regular census processes,

**[07:56:20.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006762)  
so, the effects of those shocks in 2025 and 2026, so that social protection programs and all social policies would be informed and tailored accordingly.

**[07:56:32.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006763)  
Madam Speaker, kailan po yung target natin para sa second cycle ng CBMS?

**[07:56:38.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006764)  
September 2027, Madam Speaker.

**[07:56:40.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006765)  
And gusto ko po malaman, ano yung pinaka-recent na CBMS data turnover na ibinigay ng PSA sa LGU this year?

**[07:56:49.220] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006766)  
Ano yung pinaka-recent yung turnover?

**[07:57:30.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006769)  
First quarter of 2026, Madam Speaker.

**[07:57:33.550] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006770)  
So you mean to tell me, Madam Speaker, kunwari isa po akong LGU, natanggap ko lamang ang aking CBMS data nung taong 2026, tapos 2027 maglalaan tayo ng 5 bilyon ulit, tapos kokolektahan na naman ako ng data sa 2027?

**[07:57:52.800] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006771)  
Hindi po ba, Madam Speaker, parang nakapanghinayang na isang taon lang halos napakinabangan ng data na na-collect?

**[07:57:59.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006772)  
And, since malapit na po matapos oras ko, Madam Speaker, may I just illustrate kung gaano po kaimportante ang CBMS?

**[07:58:06.560] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006773)  
And I hope our kind sponsor helps me verify this information.

**[07:58:11.200] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006774)  
Magbibigay po ako ng example.

**[07:58:13.440] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006775)  
Status of CBMS source, social pension validation.

**[07:58:17.400] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006776)  
Para po ito sa social pension ng ating mga seniors.

**[07:58:20.040] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006777)  
Total potential balance.

**[07:58:21.140] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006778)  
Beneficiaries identified from CBMS.

**[07:58:23.200] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006779)  
Batches 1 to 3.

**[07:58:24.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006780)  
Figure, 568,651.

**[07:58:28.600] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006781)  
Remaining for field validation, 251,318.

**[07:58:35.540] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006782)  
Madam Chair, sa aming pagsusuri, nagpapakita naman na gumagana mas kipapano ang ating CBMS.

**[07:58:41.900] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006783)  
Dahil may mga na-identify tayong mga beneficiaries.

**[07:58:46.240] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006784)  
Kaso lang po, binavalidate pa rin yung remaining 251,318.

**[07:58:51.140] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006785)  
Beneficiaries, hindi lamang po sa social pension for senior citizens.

**[07:58:56.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006786)  
Mas ka sa 4Ps, for example, may 107,000 households pa subject to validation.

**[07:59:03.420] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006787)  
Like I said, Madam Speaker, napakahalaga po ng CBMS dahil ito po ang listahan na pinapagbasihan natin for our social protection programs for our most vulnerable sectors.

**[07:59:15.600] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006788)  
Hindi po biro ang perang inilalaan natin para dito at napakahalaga po ito para mag-guide ang ating...

**[07:59:21.140] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006789)  
National Government Agency.

**[07:59:22.620] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006790)  
So, can we get a response from our distinguished sponsor?

**[07:59:26.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006791)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[07:59:28.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006792)  
With regards to the social pension particularly, there are multiple layers of validation.

**[07:59:35.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006793)  
For one, it's the PSA checks for the death registry.

**[07:59:40.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006794)  
At the same time, given that there's an age requirement for being able to avail of the social pension, so 60,

**[07:59:47.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006795)  
they also counter-check that with other surveys and other...

**[07:59:51.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006796)  
Registries.

**[07:59:52.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006797)  
So, that would be at least one of the reasons why validation takes so long.

**[07:59:58.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006798)  
At least on the part of the...

**[08:00:00.000] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006799)  
Before I close, Madam Speaker, may I request from our distinguished sponsor as well as the PSA,

**[08:00:06.480] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006800)  
baka pwede niyo po ako bigyan ng timeline lang kung meron kayong target dates for completion sa mga bina-validate niyo pa pong population

**[08:00:14.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006801)  
dahil napakahalaga po yung timely delivery ng data, lalong-lalo na po na maglalaan tayo ulit ng mahigit 5 bilyong piso

**[08:00:22.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006802)  
at magsasagawa na naman tayo ng census sa parating na taon.

**[08:00:29.040] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006803)  
So, pwede ko po ba makuha yung commitment ng ating sponsor pati ng PSA na bibigyan tayo ng timeline kung kailan nila tatapusin ang validation,

**[08:00:38.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006804)  
hindi lamang sa social pension, hindi lamang sa 4Ps, kundi sa lahat ng programa na may pending pa tayong validation.

**[08:00:45.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006805)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. The agency will assess and will furnish a copy to our good colleagues.

**[08:00:53.100] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006806)  
Madam Speaker, gusto ko pong ulitin and I hope hindi po ako mamisinterpret ng definition.

**[08:00:59.040] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006807)  
I want the CBMS to succeed. Napakaganda po kasi ng vision ng batas dahil napakahalaga na alam po natin kung saan may deprivation of services.

**[08:01:11.540] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006808)  
We must know where government resources must go to populations that need it the most.

**[08:01:17.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006809)  
And of course, magandang bigyan natin ang pagkakataon yung mga LGU na magplano based on actual household conditions.

**[08:01:25.880] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006810)  
Yun lamang po, Madam Speaker.

**[08:01:29.040] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-006811)  
Salamat po ako sa ating distinguished sponsor, pati po sa DepDev at sa PSA. Maraming salamat po. Magandang gabi po.

**[08:01:34.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006812)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[08:01:36.660] Deputy Speaker Maria Rachel J. Arenas** (asr-006813)  
Thank you, Honorable Abalos, Majority Leader.

**[08:01:48.040] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-006814)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Roberto Nazal of BH Party-list to interpellate on the proposed budget

**[08:01:57.560] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-006815)  
of the Department of Economy, Planning, and Development,

**[08:01:59.040] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-006816)  
including its attached agencies and corporations.

**[08:02:03.840] Deputy Speaker Maria Rachel J. Arenas** (asr-006817)  
The Honorable Roberto Nazal is recognized.

**[08:02:08.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006818)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, distinguished sponsor. Good evening to the DepDev.

**[08:02:15.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006819)  
Madam Chair, through you, do you agree, distinguished sponsor, that when government spending is significantly delayed

**[08:02:25.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006820)  
or falls short of program,

**[08:02:29.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006821)  
that can slow economic growth?

**[08:02:32.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006822)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. We agree.

**[08:02:36.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006823)  
Every year, we have unobligated, unused, and reverted appropriations.

**[08:02:43.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006824)  
Does DepDev compute the economic cost?

**[08:02:48.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006825)  
Not just the unused money, but the projects, jobs, services, and economic activity that were delayed?

**[08:02:55.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006826)  
Thank you.

**[08:02:59.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006827)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, there is a competition with regards to primary infrastructure,

**[08:03:14.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006828)  
the effects of delays in primary infrastructure projects, and also social programs.

**[08:03:21.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006829)  
Madam Speaker.

**[08:03:22.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006830)  
Thank you for that, distinguished sponsor.

**[08:03:26.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006831)  
Madam Chair, and do you show the...

**[08:03:27.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006832)  
Madam Chair, and do you show the...

**[08:03:28.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006833)  
Madam Chair, and do you show this to the agencies concerned?

**[08:03:32.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006834)  
Does DepDev, minapakita po ba ito sa mga ahensya?

**[08:03:37.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006835)  
And do we tell them what their failure to implement is costing the economy?

**[08:03:47.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006836)  
Okay.

**[08:03:49.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006837)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[08:03:51.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006838)  
These are discussed with the agencies.

**[08:03:53.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006839)  
This is also reported to the President through the Economic Development Council.

**[08:03:56.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006840)  
Okay.

**[08:03:58.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006841)  
Thank you for that, distinguished sponsor.

**[08:04:02.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006842)  
Natsi-check po ba natin, distinguished sponsor, Madam Chair, kung ano yung bottlenecks?

**[08:04:09.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006843)  
And once identified, do we recommend what the executive and the legislature needs to do to help departments address them?

**[08:04:25.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006844)  
Yes, these are checked, Madam Speaker.

**[08:04:28.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006845)  
So, kapag may cabinet meeting po, do we flag the repeat underspenders and explain what their failure to implement is costing the economy?

**[08:04:41.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006846)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, the level of the cabinet.

**[08:04:43.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006847)  
Do we have the data kung sino po ang laging underspending?

**[08:04:51.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006848)  
And can you give a sample kung ano po ang nagiging cost nito sa economy?

**[08:04:56.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006849)  
Okay.

**[08:04:58.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006850)  
Madam Speaker, just for those that fall within the purview of the DEPDEV, which is the primary infrastructure programs and the social programs,

**[08:05:19.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006851)  
but in terms of agency per agency, that would be under the DBM?

**[08:05:24.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006852)  
For example, in 2020...

**[08:05:28.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006853)  
In 2025, Madam Chair, ang dami po natin naging underspending at marami po tayo naging FISARO under sa DPWH, for example.

**[08:05:42.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006854)  
Ano po ang naging economic effect nung underspending natin sa DPWH noong 2025?

**[08:05:56.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006855)  
Okay.

**[08:05:58.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006856)  
And can you cite the economic indicators na naapektuhan?

**[08:06:05.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006857)  
As per the DEPDEV, if these projects would have been implemented in a timely manner, we would have grown an additional 1%.

**[08:06:16.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006858)  
1% in terms of GDP?

**[08:06:20.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006859)  
In terms of GDP.

**[08:06:21.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006860)  
Thank you for that, Madam Sponsor.

**[08:06:23.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006861)  
Thank you, Madam Sponsor.

**[08:06:25.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006862)  
How about implementation?

**[08:06:28.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006863)  
Every year, we see unfilled positions and heavy reliance on JOs and job orders and contract of service workers.

**[08:06:42.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006864)  
The DEPDEV study, whether these recurring staffing issues are constraining government implementation and economic growth.

**[08:06:51.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006865)  
And if this happens year after year,

**[08:06:56.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006866)  
shouldn't there be a greater urgency?

**[08:06:58.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006867)  
Should we find a government-wide solution?

**[08:07:09.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006868)  
In terms of the unfilled positions and the effect of these unfilled positions with regards to performances of agencies,

**[08:07:20.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006869)  
that would not be under the purview of the DEPDEV.

**[08:07:23.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006870)  
However, with regards to the job orders,

**[08:07:26.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006871)  
and those workers for contracts of service,

**[08:07:32.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006872)  
the DEPDEV has issued an EO recommending for social protections for them.

**[08:07:37.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006873)  
Again, sorry.

**[08:07:39.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006874)  
Can you repeat that, Madam Sponsor?

**[08:07:40.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006875)  
The DEPDEV, so, the DEPDEV cannot,

**[08:07:45.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006876)  
DEPDEV doesn't assess the effects of unfilled positions and JOs and the COSs

**[08:07:51.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006877)  
with regards to the implementing capacity of agencies,

**[08:07:55.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006878)  
agencies.

**[08:07:56.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006879)  
But, it has issued an executive order recommending for social protections for workers who are job orders,

**[08:08:04.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006880)  
contracts of service.

**[08:08:05.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006881)  
Hindi po ba dapat tingnan din po natin ito?

**[08:08:09.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006882)  
And it's about time we look at it because, first and foremost,

**[08:08:13.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006883)  
yung JOs and COS po, ang dami po nating posisyon na laging hindi nafi-fill up na

**[08:08:21.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006884)  
mga permanent positions.

**[08:08:23.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006885)  
And yung JOs and COS,

**[08:08:26.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006886)  
they remain people who have no security of tenure.

**[08:08:31.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006887)  
And siyempre, that will affect,

**[08:08:33.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006888)  
if I were in their position,

**[08:08:36.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006889)  
and wala po akong titignan na magiging future ko,

**[08:08:41.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006890)  
unstable po yung aking kinabukasan,

**[08:08:44.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006891)  
hindi po ba makakaapekto yun sa productivity?

**[08:08:47.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006892)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, it will.

**[08:08:49.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006893)  
Given that, of course, livelihood and security.

**[08:08:52.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006894)  
And this is a problem year in and year out.

**[08:08:57.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006895)  
Lagi po tayong ganito karami ang posisyon na dapat fill upan.

**[08:09:01.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006896)  
Pero

**[08:09:02.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006897)  
napakadami namang JOs and COS,

**[08:09:05.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006898)  
tapos hindi po napupuno yung mga posisyon.

**[08:09:09.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006899)  
And I'm sure,

**[08:09:11.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006900)  
the DepDev will agree that

**[08:09:13.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006901)  
this will affect

**[08:09:15.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006902)  
the productivity,

**[08:09:18.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006903)  
not only of the people

**[08:09:20.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006904)  
in those positions,

**[08:09:22.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006905)  
but also of the departments,

**[08:09:23.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006906)  
Kasi hindi po nila napupuno yung

**[08:09:24.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006907)  
kailangan

**[08:09:26.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006913)  
nilang punuin na trabaho.

**[08:09:28.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006914)  
Na posisyon.

**[08:09:30.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006915)  
Do you agree, Madam Sponsor?

**[08:09:32.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006916)  
Yes. Madam Chair.

**[08:09:33.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006917)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, we agree.

**[08:09:35.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006918)  
The agency agrees.

**[08:09:37.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006919)  
However, as of now,

**[08:09:40.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006920)  
this will not fall under the mandate of the DEF-DEF.

**[08:09:42.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006921)  
But what will the DEF-DEF do

**[08:09:45.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006922)  
moving forward?

**[08:09:56.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006923)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[08:10:08.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006924)  
The DEF-DEF is part of

**[08:10:09.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006925)  
the Committee on Drafting the IRRs

**[08:10:14.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006926)  
and the Guidelines for the Government Optimization Act.

**[08:10:18.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006927)  
This might be one of the considerations

**[08:10:21.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006928)  
that we will consider

**[08:10:23.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006929)  
for the productivity

**[08:10:26.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006930)  
of our workers.

**[08:10:27.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006931)  
And baka po, it's about time

**[08:10:28.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006932)  
that we measure this also

**[08:10:30.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006933)  
so that we can definitely tell

**[08:10:33.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006934)  
the Executive

**[08:10:34.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006935)  
or inform the Executive

**[08:10:36.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006936)  
kung ano po ang epekto

**[08:10:38.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006937)  
nung tuloy-tuloy po

**[08:10:39.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006938)  
na hindi po nila pinupuno yung posisyon.

**[08:10:42.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006939)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[08:10:44.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006940)  
Definitely, this will be one of the vehicles

**[08:10:46.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006941)  
in which we can raise this issue.

**[08:10:51.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006942)  
I'd like to continue to my next question.

**[08:10:54.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006943)  
On right-of-way,

**[08:10:56.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006944)  
Madam Chair, Madam Sponsor,

**[08:11:01.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006945)  
isang kongkreto pong halimbawa

**[08:11:04.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006946)  
ay yung right-of-way, no?

**[08:11:06.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006947)  
We were informed in a meeting with DPWH

**[08:11:09.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006948)  
that for BIT,

**[08:11:13.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006949)  
SIPAG, and convergence projects,

**[08:11:16.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006950)  
the LGU is expected to finance

**[08:11:18.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006951)  
the ROW, yung right-of-way.

**[08:11:22.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006952)  
So ibig sabihin, for a project funded

**[08:11:24.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006953)  
in the national

**[08:11:25.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006954)  
G20,

**[08:11:25.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006955)  
GAA, and implemented by DPWH,

**[08:11:31.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006956)  
anong posisyon ng DEPDEV,

**[08:11:33.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006957)  
sino po ba talaga

**[08:11:34.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006958)  
ang dapat mag-finance ng ROW?

**[08:11:37.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006959)  
National Government or LGU?

**[08:11:40.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006960)  
Kasi ang sabi po sa amin,

**[08:11:43.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006961)  
pag

**[08:11:43.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006962)  
under BIP, SIPAG,

**[08:11:46.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006963)  
or convergence,

**[08:11:48.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006964)  
ang dapat daw yung LGU.

**[08:11:53.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006965)  
Madam Speaker,

**[08:11:54.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006966)  
the position of the DEPDEV at least,

**[08:11:55.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006967)  
if it's a national project,

**[08:11:58.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006968)  
then it should be

**[08:11:59.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-006969)  
funded nationally.

**[08:12:01.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006970)  
Then, can we please come up with a

**[08:12:04.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006971)  
can we come up with a

**[08:12:06.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006972)  
siguro, I don't know kung anong pwede natin

**[08:12:08.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006973)  
ilabas,

**[08:12:09.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006974)  
a recommendation

**[08:12:11.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006975)  
na

**[08:12:13.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006976)  
baka dapat po,

**[08:12:16.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006977)  
kasi ang nangyayari sa atin,

**[08:12:19.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006978)  
any project

**[08:12:20.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006979)  
under BIP, SIPAG, and convergence,

**[08:12:22.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006980)  
hindi po siya natutuloy.

**[08:12:25.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006981)  
Dahil, ang laging nagiging issue

**[08:12:28.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006982)  
ay sasabihin nila, LGU ang dapat magbayad.

**[08:12:30.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006983)  
So, nagiging problema po yung

**[08:12:32.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006984)  
right of way.

**[08:12:36.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006985)  
For me,

**[08:12:40.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006986)  
if it is sa national,

**[08:12:41.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006987)  
tama po yung sinabi nyo, and I agree,

**[08:12:44.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006988)  
para sa amin po, pagka national kasi,

**[08:12:46.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006989)  
ang ga-a, dapat ang

**[08:12:48.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006990)  
right of way, dapat sa

**[08:12:50.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006991)  
DPWH, dapat sa National Government.

**[08:12:55.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006992)  
Meron na po bang final IRR

**[08:12:57.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006993)  
ang ARO Act?

**[08:13:25.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007010)  
As per the agency, Madam Speaker,

**[08:13:35.660] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007011)  
this would, the lead agency

**[08:13:37.260] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007012)  
would be the DPWH.

**[08:13:38.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007013)  
So, we're confirming, we'll get back to you,

**[08:13:41.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007014)  
distinguished colleague.

**[08:13:43.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007015)  
Thank you.

**[08:13:45.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007016)  
Kung wala po pa po kasi,

**[08:13:47.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007017)  
can DEV-DEV

**[08:13:49.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007018)  
commit

**[08:13:50.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007019)  
that this issue should be expressly

**[08:13:53.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007020)

**[08:13:55.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007021)  
particularly,

**[08:13:55.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007022)  
for BIP, SIPAG, and Convergence projects.

**[08:13:59.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007023)  
Yes, we agree, Madam Speaker.

**[08:14:04.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007025)  
And kasi nga po, if LGUs are not supposed to finance the ROW, then bakit po yun ang understanding ng DPWH?

**[08:14:14.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007035)  
Sana po malinaw na natin para po maiwasan na itong issue na ito.

**[08:14:19.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007041)

**[08:14:50.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007093)

**[08:14:57.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007094)  
But with the other infrastructure...

**[08:15:00.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007095)  
projects that would be under the

**[08:15:01.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007096)  
implementing agency? Madam Speaker.

**[08:15:04.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007097)  
Then how can we say

**[08:15:06.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007098)  
Madam Speaker,

**[08:15:08.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007099)  
Madam Sponsor,

**[08:15:10.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007100)  
na we are monitoring kung ano po yung

**[08:15:12.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007101)  
mga delayed projects na hindi na i-implement

**[08:15:14.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007102)

**[08:15:16.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007103)  
Because this is part

and parcel of what

generate

**[08:15:26.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007109)  
benefit for

**[08:15:29.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007110)

Kung ito po ay

cause of delay,

magkano po ang nagiging

effect nito

**[08:15:39.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007115)  
sa ating ekonomiya?

**[08:15:59.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007116)  
Madam Speaker,

**[08:16:06.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007117)  
really, well given

**[08:16:07.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007118)  
that DEPDEV only has purview for

flagship program. But

**[08:16:15.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007122)  
the rest of the projects

**[08:16:22.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007125)  
But shouldn't we be

**[08:16:23.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007126)  
monitoring this?

**[08:16:25.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007127)  
Madam Sponsor?

**[08:16:26.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007128)  
Madam Sponsor?

**[08:27:30.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007419)

So,

that is the reason why

**[08:28:40.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007454)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[08:28:42.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007455)  
The

**[08:28:44.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007456)  
Good Secretary assures that if there are

**[08:28:46.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007457)  
requests for

**[08:28:48.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007458)  
for example, for the

**[08:28:50.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007459)

**[08:28:52.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007460)  
these types of issues,

**[08:28:54.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007461)

**[08:28:56.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007462)

So,

**[08:29:00.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007464)  
ito po ang nais kong

**[08:29:02.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007465)  
tingnan nyo rin po.

**[08:29:04.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007466)  
Efficient healthcare

**[08:29:06.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007467)  
frees household income.

**[08:29:08.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007468)  
Kung hindi kailangan ubusin ng

**[08:29:10.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007469)  
pamilya ang savings nila sa hospitalization,

**[08:29:12.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007470)  
that money can

**[08:29:14.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007471)  
go to education, housing, business,

**[08:29:16.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007472)  
investment, or consumption

**[08:29:18.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007473)  
that makes the economy move.

**[08:29:20.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007474)  
So, ang tanong po natin is

**[08:29:22.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007475)  
do we quantify

**[08:29:24.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007476)  
the effect of inadequate

**[08:29:26.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007477)

on out-of-pocket spending,

**[08:29:30.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007479)  
household savings,

**[08:29:32.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007480)  
consumption, productivity,

**[08:29:34.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007481)  
and economic growth?

**[08:29:48.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007482)  
May time, please.

**[08:29:54.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007483)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[08:29:56.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007484)  
Thank you.

**[08:30:00.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007485)  
Madam Speaker, the Philippine Institute of Development Studies has a health economics program.

**[08:30:13.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007486)  
There are multiple studies that they are conducting with regards to this issue.

**[08:30:19.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007487)  
The agency, the DepDev, will be happy to furnish copies to our distinguished colleagues.

**[08:30:26.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007488)  
Thank you for that, Madam Chair, Madam Sponsor.

**[08:30:30.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007489)  
My next question is, kasi ito po, magkakadugtong po ito eh, so please bear with me.

**[08:30:37.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007490)  
Do you also compare the economic cost against whatever the national government gains?

**[08:30:42.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007491)  
Kasi po, dinidelay natin yung pondo.

**[08:30:45.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007492)  
So, most likely, because cash is fungible, we are using it elsewhere instead of dinadala natin sa PhilHealth.

**[08:30:53.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007493)  
So, do we compute?

**[08:30:58.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007494)  
Do we compare the economic cost?

**[08:31:00.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007495)  
Do we compare the economic cost against whatever the national government gains by retaining the money, reducing borrowing, or using the fiscal space elsewhere?

**[08:32:15.430] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007504)  
Madam Speaker, the PIDS and also the other attachments that will see the records if we have assessments of these trade-offs.

**[08:32:26.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007505)  
Thank you. Thank you for that. Sana po tignan natin kasi napaka-importante.

**[08:32:31.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007506)  
Okay, let me move to my next question on the HEDF.

**[08:32:35.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007507)  
Madam Chair, magkano exactly ang HEDF balance still with the Bureau of Treasury?

**[08:32:41.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007508)  
Would we know that? Meron po ba tayong details on that?

**[08:32:46.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007509)  
Can we repeat the abbreviation, Madam Speaker?

**[08:32:51.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007510)  
Magkano exactly ang HEDF balance?

**[08:32:55.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007511)  
Okay.

**[08:32:55.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007512)  
Still with the Bureau of Treasury?

**[08:32:58.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007513)  
Oh.

**[08:32:59.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007514)  
Ito po yung sa CHED.

**[08:33:02.260] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007515)  
That would be under the DOF, Madam Speaker.

**[08:33:05.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007516)  
No, the reason why I'm asking is because

**[08:33:08.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007517)  
RA 7722 says CHED shall appoint a reputable government financial institution as portfolio manager for the HEDF.

**[08:33:19.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007518)  
Yun po yung Republic Act.

**[08:33:20.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007519)  
Yet the funds were transferred to Treasury pursuant to E0338.

**[08:33:25.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007520)  
An executive order cannot supersede a law passed by Congress.

**[08:33:30.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007521)  
So, ang tanong po, why is the government allowing this apparent violation of the law to continue?

**[08:33:36.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007522)  
Is this fiscal convenience because the national government needs the money elsewhere or wants to reduce borrowing?

**[08:33:45.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007523)  
Madam Speaker, the Bureau of Treasury is an attached agency of the Department of Finance.

**[08:33:51.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007524)  
The reason why this is important, Madam Chair, distinguished sponsor,

**[08:33:58.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007525)  
has DEPDev computed whether that is actually a better economic decision?

**[08:34:03.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007526)  
Kasi parang ang nangyari po,

**[08:34:04.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007527)  
iniwan natin, kaysa sa nasa CHED po yung pera,

**[08:34:09.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007528)  
managed by a private institution, ang ginawa po,

**[08:34:14.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007529)  
nag-issue po tayo ng executive order, tapos nilagay po natin sa Bureau of Treasury.

**[08:34:20.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007530)  
So, ang nangyayari po, CHED,

**[08:34:22.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007531)  
hindi po nababayaran ng CHED yung mga obligasyon niya sa mga state universities.

**[08:34:31.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007536)  
Ngayon, meron pong economic effect yan.

**[08:34:34.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007537)  
Ano yung economic effect niyan?

**[08:34:36.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007538)  
Hindi po nabibigyan ng, maraming hindi na-accommodate na college scholars

**[08:34:42.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007539)  
or students yung mga SUCs kasi yung pera ng CHED nasa Bureau of Treasury.

**[08:34:51.840] Deputy Speaker Maria Rachel J. Arenas** (asr-007540)  
Honorable Nazal, please wrap up.

**[08:34:56.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007541)  
So, that's something lang po na I would like to, for health, for education, sana po na mamesure din po natin.

**[08:35:04.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007542)  
Let me go to my next, Madam Chair, does the DEV-DEV set a long-term objective towards a balanced budget?

**[08:35:14.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007543)  
Meron po ba tayong long-term plan na eventually, tinitignan po natin, we will achieve a balanced budget?

**[08:35:21.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007544)  
Are we gunning for that?

**[08:35:25.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007545)  
Kasi po, pataas ng pataas ang utang natin, while at the same time, marami tayong appropriations na hindi nagagamit.

**[08:35:32.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007546)  
Are we borrowing partly to support a level of appropriations that government cannot even implement?

**[08:35:42.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007547)  
Madam Speaker, well, at least on the part of DEV-DEV, in terms of a balanced budget,

**[08:35:48.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007548)  
it just assesses the macroeconomic outlook.

**[08:35:51.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007549)  
It gives the recommendation to the DBCC.

**[08:35:55.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007550)  
But in terms of balanced budget in prioritizing critical sectors, that's something that the DEV-DEV does.

**[08:36:03.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007551)  
It also assesses our compliance to sustainable development goals.

**[08:36:09.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007552)  
That's one way in which we can prioritize critical sectors in health, in education, and all of the related sectors.

**[08:36:19.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007553)  
The reason why I'm asking,

**[08:36:21.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007554)  
asking this,

**[08:36:22.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007555)  
kasi wala po ba tayong end goal in sight?

**[08:36:25.060] Deputy Speaker Maria Rachel J. Arenas** (asr-007556)  
Your time has ended already.

**[08:36:26.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007557)  
Just, almost done, ma'am.

**[08:36:28.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007558)  
Almost done.

**[08:36:30.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007559)  
So, because I understand that deficits can make sense.

**[08:36:33.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007560)  
Kasi right now, we're deficit spending.

**[08:36:36.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007561)  
If we borrow for productive investments that generate growth,

**[08:36:39.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007562)  
but if we borrow and the money cannot be implemented effectively,

**[08:36:44.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007563)  
doesn't that weaken the justification for additional debt?

**[08:36:47.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007564)  
So, yun po ang isa sa gusto kong tingnan.

**[08:36:49.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007565)  
Secondly,

**[08:36:51.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007566)  
has DEV-DEV modeled what level of deficit is actually optimal for the Philippines?

**[08:36:56.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007567)  
And what would it take eventually to balance the budget without damaging growth and essential services?

**[08:37:02.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007568)  
So, wala po ba tayong long-term plan?

**[08:37:14.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007569)  
Madam Speaker, the DEV-DEV is involved with at least the revisions and updating of the medium-term fiscal framework.

**[08:37:21.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007570)  
So, these are challenges and issues that the DEV-DEV also considers.

**[08:37:27.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007571)  
So, okay. Thank you.

**[08:37:29.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007572)  
Madam Chair, just a quick manifestation to end my interpolation.

**[08:37:35.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007573)  
Planning should not stop with setting targets.

**[08:37:38.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007574)  
For me, DEV-DEV should also know what is preventing government from implementing them,

**[08:37:44.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007575)  
what that failure costs the economy,

**[08:37:46.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007576)  
and what government needs to do to fix it.

**[08:37:49.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007577)  
Every decision,

**[08:37:50.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007578)  
to spend,

**[08:37:51.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007579)  
has a cost.

**[08:37:52.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007580)  
But every decision not to spend

**[08:37:54.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007581)  
or to delay spending

**[08:37:56.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007582)  
also has a cost.

**[08:37:58.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007583)  
I hope DEV-DEV will be much more proactive in monitoring the spending

**[08:38:02.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007584)  
and implementation performance of our agencies,

**[08:38:06.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007585)  
identifying bottlenecks early

**[08:38:08.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007586)  
and recommending to both the Executive and Congress

**[08:38:11.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007587)  
what needs to be done

**[08:38:12.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007588)  
before these problems translate into slower growth.

**[08:38:16.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-007589)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[08:38:18.240] Deputy Speaker Maria Rachel J. Arenas** (asr-007590)  
[unclear] Leader.
