**[08:53:37.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007798)  
Tapos sa GAA, 2% lang pala yung ma-approve.

**[08:53:43.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007799)  
Okay.

**[08:53:43.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007800)  
So, anong epekto pagka ganun?

**[08:53:47.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007801)  
Sa DepDev, ano yung epekto pagka ganun ang ginagawa ng Kongreso?

**[08:53:53.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007802)  
Madam Speaker, going back to what was mentioned a while ago,

**[08:53:59.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007803)  
given the delays in public infrastructure spending,

**[08:54:02.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007804)  
if we implemented those in a timely manner,

**[08:54:06.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007805)  
we would have grown.

**[08:54:07.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007806)  
One percentage point higher.

**[08:54:09.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007807)  
So, that's one estimate that the DepDev has.

**[08:54:15.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007808)  
But in terms of the DOTR projects,

**[08:54:19.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007809)  
like the DepDev only has purview over the IFPs.

**[08:54:23.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007810)  
So, there are 60 IFPs for DOTR.

**[08:54:28.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007811)  
If it's okay with our distinguished colleague,

**[08:54:31.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007812)  
we can give a copy as well as the status of these projects.

**[08:54:35.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007813)  
Or I can enumerate if we're good.

**[08:54:37.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007814)  
If my colleague wants.

**[08:54:40.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007815)  
Okay.

**[08:54:40.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007816)  
So, I am told...

**[08:54:42.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007817)  
Madam Chair, baka pwedeng...

**[08:54:45.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007818)  
May tanong lang po ako sa regarding the parliamentary status.

**[08:54:53.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007819)  
If I may ask first,

**[08:54:55.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007820)  
pwede mo matigil lang po yung oras?

**[08:55:01.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007821)  
Ayaw, itigil yung oras.

**[08:55:03.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007822)  
No, I am told that now the interpolation will include both...

**[08:55:07.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007823)  
DepDev and DBM?

**[08:55:11.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007824)  
Ganun po ba yun?

**[08:55:15.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007825)  
Okay.

**[08:55:15.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007826)  
O hindi.

**[08:55:16.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007827)  
You are not defending DBM budget.

**[08:55:19.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007828)  
Just the DepDev budget.

**[08:55:21.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007829)  
Ah, DepDev budget.

**[08:55:22.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007830)  
Okay, it's clear.

**[08:55:22.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007831)  
Thank you.

**[08:55:23.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007832)  
Thank you.

**[08:55:24.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007833)  
Alright.

**[08:55:25.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007834)  
Alright.

**[08:55:26.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007835)  
So, ano po nangyayari?

**[08:55:28.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007836)  
So, obviously, pagka hindi pinunduhan,

**[08:55:30.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007837)  
hindi ma-implement.

**[08:55:33.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007838)  
Tama po.

**[08:55:34.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007839)  
Okay.

**[08:55:36.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007840)  
However,

**[08:55:36.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007841)  
meron akong question dyan

**[08:55:38.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007842)  
because

**[08:55:38.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007843)  
in the NEP,

**[08:55:42.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007844)  
yung mga foreign-assisted projects na ganyan,

**[08:55:45.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007845)  
they are tied to specific loans.

**[08:55:48.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007846)  
Tama po ba?

**[08:55:49.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007847)  
Perfected loans.

**[08:55:50.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007848)  
Correct?

**[08:55:52.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007849)  
Correct?

**[08:55:53.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007850)  
Tama po ba?

**[08:55:55.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007851)  
Yung nasa NEP,

**[08:55:57.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007852)  
na foreign-assisted project ng DOTR,

**[08:56:00.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007853)  
meron ng loan agreement yun.

**[08:56:04.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007854)  
Tama?

**[08:56:04.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007855)  
Tama?

**[08:56:06.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007856)  
Not all projects, Madam Speaker.

**[08:56:10.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007857)  
But most IFPs for foreign-assisted would be tied to official development assistance loans.

**[08:56:18.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007858)  
So, as a general rule, yung nilalagay sa NEP, yun ay yung meron ng loan na.

**[08:56:22.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007859)  
Sigurado na, nandyan na.

**[08:56:23.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007860)  
Hindi yung mag-ne-negotiate pa lang kami, lalagay na namin sa NEP.

**[08:56:27.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007861)  
Di ba? Hindi ganon?

**[08:56:28.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007862)  
Yes.

**[08:56:29.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007863)  
Nando na yung loan.

**[08:56:30.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007864)  
Okay.

**[08:56:31.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007865)  
So, however, my question now is,

**[08:56:33.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007866)  
pagka ginawa yan ng Kongreso,

**[08:56:36.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007867)  
proposal was 151 billion,

**[08:56:42.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007868)  
and then the approved in the GAA was only a fraction of that,

**[08:56:46.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007869)  
2% of that.

**[08:56:49.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007870)  
Ang tanyari, shouldn't that then, dahil natanggal na yung...

**[08:56:54.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007871)  
The effect there is that the source of funding is no longer available, correct?

**[08:57:01.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007872)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[08:57:02.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007873)  
In other words,

**[08:57:03.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007874)  
Congress cannot use the...

**[08:57:06.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007875)  
The freed up, let's call that freed up fiscal space, yun ba ang tawag doon?

**[08:57:14.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007876)  
Okay.

**[08:57:16.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007877)  
To fund other projects.

**[08:57:18.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007878)  
Di ba hindi pwede yun?

**[08:57:19.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007879)  
No, Madam Speaker.

**[08:57:20.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007880)  
Hindi pwede, di ba?

**[08:57:21.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007881)  
Nasabi natin, teka, 150 dapat sa foreign assisted, pero ginawasan natin ng 140.

**[08:57:29.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007882)  
Therefore, we have freed up 140 for other projects.

**[08:57:33.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007883)  
Di ba hindi pwede yun?

**[08:57:35.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007884)  
No.

**[08:57:36.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007885)  
No, Madam Speaker.

**[08:57:37.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007886)  
Hindi pwede.

**[08:57:37.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007887)  
Eh, pero yun ang ginagawa ng Kongreso all the time.

**[08:57:41.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007888)  
Di ba may mali doon?

**[08:57:43.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007889)  
So, in other words,

**[08:57:44.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007890)  
if when Congress, or if Congress specifically reduces the appropriation for

**[08:57:53.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007891)  
foreign assisted projects with perfected loan agreements,

**[08:57:59.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007892)  
pagka binawasan yun ng Kongreso, in terms of pag hindi inappropriate yun ng Kongreso,

**[08:58:05.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007893)  
then that should...

**[08:58:06.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007894)  
reduce the total budget.

**[08:58:10.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007895)  
Kung baga, yung, kunyari, 7.2, dapat kung binawasan mo ng 100B na foreign assisted,

**[08:58:18.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007896)  
dapat 7.1 na lang ang ceiling.

**[08:58:21.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007897)  
Hindi ba ganun dapat ang epekto nun?

**[08:58:24.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007898)  
You get my point?

**[08:58:25.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007899)  
Madam, correct?

**[08:58:30.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007900)  
Could you please place that on the record?

**[08:58:35.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007901)  
Well, Madam Speaker,

**[08:58:36.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007902)  
if it's in the GAA,

**[08:58:39.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007903)  
then it should be,

**[08:58:42.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007904)  
the funding for that particular project is,

**[08:58:46.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007905)  
for that particular project, you can't modify.

**[08:58:49.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007906)  
Well, I think what I'm pointing out is, in fact,

**[08:58:52.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007907)  
that ganun nga nangyayari, no?

**[08:58:54.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007908)  
I mean, for many years,

**[08:58:57.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007909)  
ginagawa ng Kongreso, binabawasan ng malaki

**[08:58:59.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007910)  
yung DOTR, foreign assisted projects,

**[08:59:03.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007911)  
and these are specifically,

**[08:59:06.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007912)  
tied to a source, the loan,

**[08:59:10.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007913)  
tapos, the freed up space is used for, you know,

**[08:59:17.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007914)  
for insertion, congressional insertion.

**[08:59:22.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007915)  
Yung mga proyektong dinadagdag.

**[08:59:26.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007916)  
So, specifically,

**[08:59:28.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007917)  
pinakamalala yan nung years 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025.

**[08:59:34.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007918)  
Ayan din yung panahon na pinakamalala,

**[08:59:35.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007919)  
yung flood control

**[08:59:37.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007920)  
insertions, diba?

**[08:59:40.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007921)  
So, yun ang point ko.

**[08:59:41.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007922)  
Okay. That is the point

**[08:59:44.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007923)  
I'm making. And now,

**[08:59:45.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007924)  
the question I am asking

**[08:59:47.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007925)  
this Congress is, gagawin na naman ba yun

**[08:59:50.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007926)  
ng Kongresong ito?

**[08:59:54.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007927)  
Pwede pa bang gawin yun ng Kongresong ito?

**[09:00:00.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007928)  
Well, Madam Speaker, well, not pertaining to the budget of the DepDev.

**[09:00:04.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007929)  
That's precisely the reason why we're instituting a lot of the reforms that we have.

**[09:00:10.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-007930)  
We're trying to be transparent and accountable.

**[09:00:11.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007931)  
Alright. So nagre-reforman na, so dapat hindi na pwede.

**[09:00:16.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007932)  
So, klaro, hindi porke 2027, nasa 232 billion ang proposed foreign-assisted projects.

**[09:00:27.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007933)  
Projects. Tapos kung titignan nyo yung item nyo, nakalagay talaga doon yung loan agreement.

**[09:00:32.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007934)  
Meron pang serial number, etc., etc., nung loan agreement.

**[09:00:36.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007935)  
So hindi na pwedeng galawin ito as a source for congressional amendments or insertions.

**[09:00:44.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007936)  
Okay, that's clear.

**[09:00:46.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007937)  
Another, the other, yan naman, yung isa naman.

**[09:00:50.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007938)  
Yan, ang isa pang paborito over the years, PS, personnel services.

**[09:00:57.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007939)  
Specifically here, we're focusing on miscellaneous personnel benefits fund.

**[09:01:04.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007940)  
Okay, ano yung miscellaneous personnel?

**[09:01:06.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007941)  
Para po yan sa dagdag na sweldo, dagdag na binipisyo, o new hiring, new plantilla positions ng gobyerno.

**[09:01:15.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007942)  
Okay, and what do we see?

**[09:01:17.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007943)  
Again, we see the same pattern.

**[09:01:19.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007944)  
In 2016, doon tayo nagsimula, kung ano yung pinroposed sa NEP na MPBF noon,

**[09:01:25.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007945)  
yun din ang aprobahan.

**[09:01:27.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007946)  
Anong kongreso?

**[09:01:31.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007947)  
Pero, I mean, even up to, well, ayan, meron na nangyari, no?

**[09:01:39.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007948)  
Pero makikita talaga natin, starting in 2020, anong year ba ito?

**[09:01:46.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007949)  
2022, and especially 2023, we see the same pattern.

**[09:01:52.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007950)  
NEP, MPBF is 62 billion.

**[09:01:57.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007951)  
But the GAA, MPBF is, 2023, GAA is 26 billion.

**[09:02:06.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007952)  
70% lower.

**[09:02:09.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007953)  
Binawasan ng 70%.

**[09:02:11.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007954)  
Tapos, and so on.

**[09:02:14.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007955)  
Again, ang pinakamalala, no?

**[09:02:18.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007956)  
So, kailangan matalasa kay Saisaya na ang pinakamalalang mga abuso sa budget, 2024.

**[09:02:25.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007957)  
No?

**[09:02:27.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007958)  
So, MPBF then was 135, but approved in GAA is 29 billion.

**[09:02:37.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007959)  
29 billion.

**[09:02:39.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007960)  
Okay.

**[09:02:40.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007961)  
So, ano nangyari doon sa pondo na yun?

**[09:02:42.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007962)  
No?

**[09:02:43.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007963)  
Ano nangyari?

**[09:02:43.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007964)  
Yun yung sinatawag dito colloquially na ginawang source para sa mga insertions.

**[09:02:51.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007965)  
Tama po ba?

**[09:02:52.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007966)  
Am I correct in my analysis?

**[09:02:54.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007967)  
Madam sponsor?

**[09:02:57.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007968)  
The DEV cannot confirm.

**[09:03:00.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007969)  
The DEV cannot confirm.

**[09:03:03.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007970)  
Okay.

**[09:03:04.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007971)  
Well, I confirm.

**[09:03:07.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007972)  
And the data shows na ganun po ang nangyari.

**[09:03:10.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007973)  
Okay.

**[09:03:11.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007974)  
So, my specific concern is with regard to MPBF for 2027.

**[09:03:18.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007975)  
As you can see, the 2027 proposed is 55 billion.

**[09:03:22.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007976)  
A little over 55 billion.

**[09:03:24.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007977)  
Mamaya.

**[09:03:26.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007978)  
Wala yata yung...

**[09:03:27.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007979)  
Wala.

**[09:03:27.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007980)  
Wala pa yung DBM.

**[09:03:28.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007981)  
Babalik sila.

**[09:03:30.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007982)  
But isang tinututukan natin dito yung ano eh.

**[09:03:33.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007983)  
Ito ang paggagalingan ng pagpapataas sa pera.

**[09:03:39.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007984)  
Personal, economic relief allowance ng mga government employees.

**[09:03:44.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007985)  
Yan.

**[09:03:44.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007986)  
Lahat kayo rito, government employees.

**[09:03:46.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007987)  
Yung iba sa inyo, siguro mas malaki ang kita.

**[09:03:48.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007988)  
Baka hindi nyo masyadong nararamdaman to.

**[09:03:50.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007989)  
Pero yung pera, yan yung 2,000 pesos a month na allowance.

**[09:03:56.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007990)  
On top of the basic pay.

**[09:04:00.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007991)  
Okay.

**[09:04:00.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007992)  
Yung pera na yan, 2,000 na yung papuyan na set at 2,000 pesos a month.

**[09:04:08.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007993)  
Okay.

**[09:04:09.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007994)

**[09:34:53.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008388)  
it is the position of the office,

**[09:34:55.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008389)  
the office of the Solicitor General,

**[09:34:56.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008390)  
abogado ng gobyerno,

**[09:34:58.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008391)  
na sinasabi nila,

**[09:35:00.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008392)  
there is no legal impediment in enforcing Wage Order No. 27.

**[09:35:06.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008393)  
The question again is,

**[09:35:08.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008394)  
even if that is very clear sa ating mga batas,

**[09:35:13.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008395)

**[09:35:25.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-008396)  
Madam Speaker, again, just on the part of DepDev,

**[09:35:30.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-008397)  
it was a collegial decision.

**[09:35:32.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008398)  
Thank you, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[09:35:36.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008399)  
Ang punto ko po, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[09:35:41.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008400)  
sa dinami-rami ko pong binanggit ng mga puntos,

**[09:35:44.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008401)  
mismong DepDev na bahagi ng NWPC

**[09:35:47.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008402)  
ay lito, una, sa mga tunay na nangyari at naganap

**[09:35:52.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008403)  
leading to the issuance of Wage Order No. 27.

**[09:35:55.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008404)  
Mas nalito pa tayo lahat dahil may existing Wage Order No. 27

**[09:36:00.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008405)  
at naglabas ng panibagong Wage Order No. 28

**[09:36:03.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008406)  
kahit labag ito sa rules mismo ng NWPC.

**[09:36:07.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008407)  
Second point, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker,

**[09:36:10.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008408)  
hindi nagkaroon ng public consultation.

**[09:36:13.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008409)  
Third point, hindi rin nagkaroon ng meeting ang NWPC

**[09:36:17.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008410)  
para pagtibayin yung Wage Order No. 28.

**[09:36:21.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008411)  
Pero binabanggit kanina, nagkaroon daw ng pulong,

**[09:36:23.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008412)  
pero hindi rin nilaman.

**[09:36:25.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008413)  
Malaman o matandaan saan sila nagpulong,

**[09:36:27.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008414)  
anong oras sila nagpulong, at merong kalituhan.

**[09:36:30.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008415)  
So, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[09:36:32.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008416)  
mas nagbibigay ito ng maraming tanong kaysa kalinawan.

**[09:36:37.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008417)  
Kaya ako po binibigyang din ito, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[09:36:41.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008418)  
malinaw po kasi na merong wage rollback na nangyari.

**[09:36:45.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008419)  
Wage Order No. 27, total wage increase amounts to P85.

**[09:36:51.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008420)  
P60 of which, the first tranche,

**[09:36:55.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008421)  
effective na since July 25.

**[09:36:58.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008422)  
That means, kapag in-implement yung Wage Order No. 27,

**[09:37:02.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008423)  
may retroactive pay ang mga manggagawang minimum wage earner

**[09:37:06.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008424)  
ng P60 starting July 25.

**[09:37:10.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008425)  
So, pumapatak ang metro, mahigit P3,000 na ang naaagaw mula sa mga manggagawa.

**[09:37:16.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008426)  
Hindi po simpleng halaga yan.

**[09:37:18.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008427)  
Malaking tulong yan para sa mga manggagawa, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[09:37:21.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008428)  
Ngayon, sa halip na tindigan,

**[09:37:25.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008429)  
ng NWPC, ng DOLE, kung ano yung kanyang mandato,

**[09:37:29.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008430)  
dahil iginawad yan ng Kongreso sa visa ng RA 6727,

**[09:37:35.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008431)  
nakikita po natin na parang isinasantabi o isinusuko

**[09:37:39.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008432)  
ng NWPC mismo kung saan pahagi ang DepDev,

**[09:37:44.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008433)  
yung kanyang responsibilidad at trabaho

**[09:37:46.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008434)  
bilang merong exclusive jurisdiction on wage determination.

**[09:37:55.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008435)  
Yun po ang punto dito, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[09:37:58.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008436)  
Mas malaking problema, because of Wage Order No. 28,

**[09:38:02.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008437)  
wala nang aasahan na retroactive pay yung mga manggagawa

**[09:38:06.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008438)  
unless otherwise clarified and cleared by the court.

**[09:38:11.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008439)  
Ang problema pa dito, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[09:38:14.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008440)  
because of Wage Order No. 28,

**[09:38:16.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008441)  
na wala na yung dagdag na P25 sa mga minimum wage earners

**[09:38:20.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008442)  
na dapat matanggap nila Enero 2027.

**[09:38:23.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008443)  
And it will produce another question.

**[09:38:28.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008444)  
Kung ang posisyon ng NWPC ay para magkaroon ng inovasyon

**[09:38:32.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008445)  
maibigay na sa mga manggagawa, yung P60,

**[09:38:35.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008446)  
ibig sabihin ba magkakaroon ng Wage Order No. 29?

**[09:38:40.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008447)  
Na magsasabi, magdagdag tayo ng panibagong P25

**[09:38:44.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008448)  
para sa mga minimum wage earners.

**[09:38:47.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008449)  
E di-veniolate na, nilabag na ng NWPC

**[09:38:51.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008450)  
yung kanyang sariling rules.

**[09:38:53.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008451)  
Naglabas ng dalawang wage order sa isang taon

**[09:38:57.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008452)  
at ngayon may question kung maglalabas ulit ng panibagong wage order.

**[09:39:01.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008453)  
Yun po ang punto dito, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[09:39:05.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008454)  
Mismong mga regional wage boards, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker,

**[09:39:09.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008455)  
ay hindi sumusunod sa mga proseso ng batas

**[09:39:12.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008456)  
na ginawa para itakda yung minimum wage natatanggap ng mga manggagawa

**[09:39:19.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008457)  
at yan po yung dahilan.

**[09:39:20.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008458)  
Mga manggagawa na tayo,

**[09:39:22.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008459)  
una, hindi nang-responsive,

**[09:39:23.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008460)

**[09:39:26.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008461)  
Ikalawa, nilalabag pa mismo ng mga ahensa ng gobyerno

**[09:39:30.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008462)  
kung ano nakasulat sa batas.

**[09:39:32.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008463)  
Kung hindi nasusunod ang batas,

**[09:39:34.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008464)  
logical lang na dapat reviewhin o repasuhin ang batas.

**[09:39:39.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008465)  
Ngayon po, kung nakikita natin na may kaguluhan na nagaganap

**[09:39:43.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008466)  
dito sa wage setting determination natin

**[09:39:46.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008467)  
as enshrined in RA 6727,

**[09:39:49.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008468)  
nakakapagtaka po, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker,

**[09:39:52.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008469)  
that the position,

**[09:39:53.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008470)  
of the Secretary of Dep-Def,

**[09:39:57.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008471)  
sabi po ay,

**[09:39:58.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008472)  
legislating a minimum wage

**[09:40:00.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008473)  
is not the right thing to do.

**[09:40:04.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008474)

**[09:40:07.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008475)

**[09:40:10.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008476)

**[09:40:12.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008477)  
kung nakikita na natin ngayon

**[09:40:14.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008478)

**[09:40:18.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008479)  
If we're going back to our history,

**[09:40:21.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008480)

**[09:40:23.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008483)

**[09:40:27.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008484)  
Sabi nung mga nagtulak niyan,

**[09:40:29.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008485)

**[09:40:32.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008486)

**[09:40:35.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008487)  
o pamumulitika

**[09:40:36.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008488)  
o pangingi-alam ng korte

**[09:40:39.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008489)  
yung wage setting determination natin.

**[09:40:42.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008490)  
That was in 1989.

**[09:40:45.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008491)  
Ngayon, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[09:40:48.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008492)  
nakikita na natin lahat ng kinatatakutan

**[09:40:51.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008493)  
ng mga gumawa ng batas noong 1989.

**[09:40:53.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008494)  
ngayon, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[09:40:54.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008495)  
na ang niyayari na ngayon.

**[09:40:57.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008496)  
Pinanghimasukan ng korte

**[09:40:59.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008497)  
yung tungkol sa sahod

**[09:41:01.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008498)  
at ikalawa, nilalabag na mismo ng gobyerno

**[09:41:04.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008499)  
kung ano ang nakasulat sa batas

**[09:41:06.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008500)  
na iginawad ng kongresong ito

**[09:41:08.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008501)  
papunta doon sa National Wages and Productivity Commission.

**[09:41:12.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008502)  
And what we're going to hear

**[09:41:14.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008503)  
from Secretary Balisakan and Dep-Dev

**[09:41:17.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008504)  
is that legislating a minimum wage

**[09:41:19.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008505)  
is not the right thing to do.

**[09:41:22.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008506)  
Is it still the position of the government?

**[09:41:23.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008508)  
Is it still the position of Dep-Dev

**[09:41:24.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008509)  
na hindi po magkaroon ng legislated wage increase

**[09:41:28.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008510)

**[09:41:33.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008511)

**[09:41:39.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008512)

**[09:42:37.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-008534)
